4.7
Lin-Tsien equation

Cost function derivation

The equation is,

\displaystyle 2u_{tx}+u_{x}u_{xx}-u_{yy}=0

\displaystyle\text{ }\Updownarrow\text{ }

\displaystyle\frac{2}{\tau\Delta x}\big(A_{x}-\textbf{1}\big)\ket{u(x,y,t+\tau)}+\big(\frac{A_{x}-\textbf{1}}{\Delta x}\big)\ket{u(x,y,t)}\big(\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)\ket{u(x,y,t)}-\cdots

\displaystyle\Bigg\{\big(\frac{A^{\dagger}_{y}-2\textbf{1}+A_{y}}{(\Delta y)^{2}}\big)\ket{u(x,y,t)}\Bigg\}=0

After representing all spatial and time derivatives with the corresponding quantum operators, the PDE takes the form,

\displaystyle\frac{2}{\tau\Delta x}\big(A_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t+\tau)}-\ket{u(x,y,t+\tau)}\big)+\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\big(A_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}-\ket{u(x,y,t)}\big)\text{ }\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}-2\ket{u(x,y,t)}\text{ }\cdots

\displaystyle+A_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\big)-\frac{1}{(\Delta y)^{2}}\big(A^{\dagger}_{y}\ket{u(x,y,t)}-2\ket{u(x,y,t)}+A_{y}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\big)=0\text{ , }

from which multiplying terms scaled in the increment of spatial derivatives produces the following sequence of rearrangements,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\big(A_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}-\ket{u(x,y,t)}\big)\text{ }\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}-2\ket{u(x,y,t)}+A_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\big)

\displaystyle\Updownarrow

\displaystyle\text{ }\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\big(A_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\cdots

\displaystyle A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}-A_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}2\ket{u(x,y,t)}+\textbf{1}-\ket{u(x,y,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}+\cdots

\displaystyle 2-\ket{u(x,y,t)}A_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\big)

\displaystyle\frac{\tau\Delta x}{2}\Bigg\{\frac{2}{\tau\Delta x}A_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t+\tau)}+\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\big(A_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}-A_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}2\ket{u(x,y,t)}+\textbf{1}-\ket{u(x,y,t)}\cdots

\displaystyle A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}+2-\ket{u(x,y,t)}A_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\big)-\frac{1}{(\Delta y)^{2}}\big(A^{\dagger}_{y}\ket{u(x,y,t)}-2\ket{u(x,y,t)}+

\displaystyle A_{y}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\big)\Bigg\}=\ket{u(x,y,t+\tau)}\text{ . }

The resulting time evolved variational state isolated above yields the cost function,

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}^{L-T}(\lambda,\lambda_{0})=\bigg|\bigg|\ket{u(x,y,t+\tau)}-\ket{u(x,y,t)}\bigg|\bigg|^{2}

\displaystyle\Updownarrow

\displaystyle\bigg|\bigg|A_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t+\tau)}+\frac{\tau}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big(A_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\text{ }\Big(\text{ }A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\text{ }\Big)\text{ }\ket{u(x,y,t)}+\cdots

\displaystyle\textbf{1}-\ket{u(x,y,t)}\Big(\text{ }A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}-A_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\text{ }\Big)\text{ }+2\text{ }\big)-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{\tau\Delta x}{2(\Delta y)^{2}}\big(A^{\dagger}_{y}\ket{u(x,y,t)}-2\ket{u(x,y,t)}+A_{y}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\big)\bigg|\bigg|^{2}

We collect terms from squaring the norm below,

\displaystyle\big(\frac{\tau}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\text{ }\big(\text{ }A_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\Big(\text{ }A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\Big)\text{ }\cdots

\displaystyle\ket{u(x,y,t)}+\textbf{1}-\ket{u(x,y,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}-\text{ }\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle\ket{u(x,y,t)}A_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}+2-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{1}{(\Delta y)^{2}}\big(A^{\dagger}_{y}\ket{u(x,y,t)}-2\ket{u(x,y,t)}+A_{y}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\big)\bigg\}^{2}\times\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }\big(\frac{\tau}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big)^{2}\text{ }\Bigg\{\text{ }\Bigg(\text{ }A_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\Big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\text{ }\Big)\text{ }\ket{u(x,y,t)}\text{ }\Bigg)^{2}+\text{ }\cdots

\displaystyle 2\text{ }\Bigg\{\text{ }A_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\Big(\text{ }A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\text{ }\Big)\ket{u(x,y,t)}\Bigg\}\times\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }\big(\text{ }\textbf{1}-\ket{u(x,y,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}-\ket{u(x,y,t)}A_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}+\text{ }2\text{ }\bigg\}+\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }\Big(\text{ }\text{ }\textbf{1}-\ket{u(x,y,t)}\text{ }A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}-\ket{u(x,y,t)}A_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}+\cdots

\displaystyle 2-\text{ }\frac{1}{(\Delta y)^{2}}\bigg(A^{\dagger}_{y}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\text{ }-\text{ }\text{ }2\ket{u(x,y,t)}+A_{y}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\big)\text{ }\Big)^{2}\text{ }\text{ . }

To obtain the desired cost function, we determine all terms in the quantum superposition resulting from the expansion of terms above. Rearrangements imply,

\displaystyle 3-\ket{u(x,y,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}-\ket{u(x,y,t)}A_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}-\frac{A^{\dagger}_{y}}{(\Delta y)^{2}}\text{ }\ket{u(x,y,t)}+\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{2}{(\Delta y)^{2}}\text{ }\ket{u(x,y,t)}-\frac{A_{y}}{(\Delta y)^{2}}\text{ }\ket{u(x,y,t)}\text{ }

\displaystyle\Updownarrow

\displaystyle 3-\bigg(\text{ }\ket{u(x,y,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}-\ket{u(x,y,t)}A_{x}-\frac{1}{(\Delta y)^{2}}\big(A^{\dagger}_{y}-2\textbf{1}+A_{y}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }\ket{u(x,y,t)}\text{ . }

Squaring the last term of the expansion for the cost function above gives,

\displaystyle\Big(\text{ }3-\big(\ket{u(x,y,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}-\ket{u(x,y,t)}A_{x}-\frac{1}{(\Delta y)^{2}}\big(A^{\dagger}_{y}-2\textbf{1}+A_{y}\big)\text{ }\big)\text{ }\ket{u(x,y,t)}\text{ }\Big)^{2}\text{ }

\displaystyle\Updownarrow

\displaystyle\Big(\text{ }3-\big(\text{ }\ket{u(x,y,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}-\ket{u(x,y,t)}A_{x}-\frac{1}{(\Delta y)^{2}}\big(\text{ }A^{\dagger}_{y}-2\textbf{1}+A_{y}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\big)\text{ }\ket{u(x,y,t)}\Big)\text{ }\times\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle\Big(\text{ }3-\bra{u(x,y,t)}\big(\text{ }\ket{u(x,y,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}-\ket{u(x,y,t)}A_{x}-\frac{1}{(\Delta y)^{2}}\text{ }\big(\text{ }A^{\dagger}_{y}-2\textbf{1}+A_{y}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\big)^{\dagger}\text{ }\Big)\text{ }\text{ , }

which is further rearranged as,

\displaystyle\text{ }9-\text{ }\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }3\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }\bra{u(x,y,t)}\Bigg(\text{ }\ket{u(x,y,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}-\ket{u(x,y,t)}A_{z}-\frac{1}{(\Delta y)^{2}}\big(\text{ }A^{\dagger}_{y}-2\textbf{1}+A_{y}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\bigg)^{\dagger}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }-\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle 3\text{ }\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }\ket{u(x,y,t)}\text{ }A^{\dagger}_{x}-\ket{u(x,y,t)}A_{x}\text{ }-\frac{1}{(\Delta y)^{2}}\big(\text{ }A^{\dagger}_{y}-2\textbf{1}\text{ }+A_{y}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\Bigg)\text{ }\ket{u(x,y,t)}+\text{ }\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle\bigg(\text{ }\ket{u(x,y,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}-\text{ }\ket{u(x,y,t)}A_{x}-\frac{1}{(\Delta y)^{2}}\big(\text{ }A^{\dagger}_{y}-2\textbf{1}\text{ }+A_{y}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }\ket{u(x,y,t)}\text{ }\times\cdots

\displaystyle\bra{u(x,y,t)}\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }\ket{u(x,y,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}-\ket{u(x,y,t)}A_{x}-\frac{1}{(\Delta y)^{2}}\big(\text{ }A^{\dagger}_{y}-2\textbf{1}\text{ }+\text{ }A_{y}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\text{ }\bigg)^{\dagger}\text{ . }

In order from the superposition above, we distribute terms amongst the superposition, and take the Hermitian conjugate of the last term,

\displaystyle 9-\bigg(\text{ }3\text{ }\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }\bra{u(x,y,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\text{ }-\text{ }\bra{u(x,y,t)}A_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}-\frac{A^{\dagger}_{y}}{(\Delta y)^{2}}\ket{u(x,y,t)}-\cdots

\displaystyle 2\ket{u(x,y,t)}+A_{y}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }-\cdots

\displaystyle 3\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }\bra{u(x,y,t)}A_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}-\bra{u(x,y,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}-\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle\frac{A^{\dagger}_{y}}{(\Delta y)^{2}}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\text{ }-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{2}{(\Delta y)^{2}}\ket{u(x,y,t)}-\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }\frac{A_{y}}{(\Delta y)^{2}}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }+\bigg(\text{ }\bra{u(x,y,t)}A_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}-\cdots

\displaystyle\bra{u(x,y,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\text{ }-\frac{A^{\dagger}_{y}}{(\Delta y)^{2}}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }\times\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle\bigg(\text{ }\ket{u(x,y,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}-\ket{u(x,y,t)}A_{x}-\frac{1}{(\Delta y)^{2}}\big(\text{ }A^{\dagger}_{y}-2\textbf{1}+A_{y}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }\ket{u(x,y,t)}\text{ }\times\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle\bra{u(x,y,t)}A_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\bra{u(x,y,t)}\text{ }A_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}-\cdots

\displaystyle\bra{u(x,y,t)}\text{ }A_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\bra{u(x,y,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}-\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle\bra{u(x,y,t)}A_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\frac{A^{\dagger}_{y}}{(\Delta y)^{2}}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\text{ }+\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle\bra{u(x,y,t)}A_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\text{ }\frac{2}{(\Delta y)^{2}}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\text{ }-\text{ }\bra{u(x,y,t)}A_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\text{ }\cdots\text{ }\text{ }

\displaystyle\frac{A_{y}}{(\Delta y)^{2}}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\text{ }-\text{ }\bra{u(x,y,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\text{ }\bra{u(x,y,t)}A_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\text{ }-\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle\text{ }\bra{u(x,y,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\times\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle\bra{u(x,y,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\text{ . }

Besides these rearrangements, additional expectation terms in the superposition are of the following form,

\displaystyle\bra{u(x,y,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\text{ }\frac{A^{\dagger}_{y}}{(\Delta y)^{2}}\ket{u(x,y,t)}-\text{ }\bra{u(x,y,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\frac{2}{(\Delta y)^{2}}\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle\ket{u(x,y,t)}\text{ }\bra{u(x,y,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\frac{A_{y}}{(\Delta y)^{2}}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\text{ }-\frac{A^{\dagger}_{y}}{(\Delta y)^{2}}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\bra{u(x,y,t)}A_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\text{ }-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{A^{\dagger}_{y}}{(\Delta y)^{2}}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\text{ }\bra{u(x,y,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}+\frac{A^{\dagger}_{y}}{(\Delta y)^{2}}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\frac{A^{\dagger}_{y}}{(\Delta y)^{2}}\ket{u(x,y,t)}-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{A^{\dagger}_{y}}{(\Delta y)^{2}}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\frac{2}{(\Delta y)^{2}}\ket{u(x,y,t)}+\frac{A^{\dagger}_{y}}{(\Delta y)^{2}}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\text{ }\frac{A_{y}}{(\Delta y)^{2}}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\text{ }\times\cdots

\displaystyle\bra{u(x,y,t)}A_{x}\text{ }A_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}-\bra{u(x,y,t)}\text{ }A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}-\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle\bra{u(x,y,t)}A_{x}\frac{A_{y}}{(\Delta y)^{2}}\text{ }+\bra{u(x,y,t)}A_{x}\text{ }\frac{2}{(\Delta y)^{2}}\text{ }-\text{ }\bra{u(x,y,t)}A_{x}\text{ }\cdots\text{ }\text{ }

\displaystyle\frac{A^{\dagger}_{y}}{(\Delta y)^{2}}\text{ }-\text{ }\bra{u(x,y,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}\text{ }A_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\text{ }-\bra{u(x,y,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}\cdots

\displaystyle A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\text{ }+\bra{u(x,y,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}\text{ }\frac{A_{y}}{(\Delta y)^{2}}-\text{ }\bra{u(x,y,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}\frac{2}{(\Delta y)^{2}}-\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle\text{ }A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}-\frac{A^{\dagger}_{y}}{(\Delta y)^{2}}A_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\text{ }-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{A^{\dagger}_{y}}{(\Delta y)^{2}}\text{ }A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\text{ }+\text{ }\bra{u(x,y,t)}\frac{1}{(\Delta y)^{2}}\times\frac{1}{(\Delta y)^{2}}\ket{u(x,y,t)}-\bra{u(x,y,t)}\frac{2}{(\Delta y)^{2}}\times\text{ }\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{A^{\dagger}_{y}}{(\Delta y)^{2}}\ket{u(x,y,t)}+\text{ }\frac{1}{(\Delta y)^{2}}\frac{1}{(\Delta y)^{2}}\text{ }\text{ . }\text{ }

Continuing, we further expand terms after distributing states from the expansion of the norm to determine the cost function whose ground state we investigate,

\displaystyle\text{ }\big(\bra{u(x,y,t)}\text{ }A_{x}A_{x}-\bra{u(x,y,t)}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}-\bra{u(x,y,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}-\bra{u(x,y,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\text{ }-A^{\dagger}_{x}-\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle\frac{A^{\dagger}_{y}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}A_{x}\text{ }-\frac{A^{\dagger}_{y}}{(\Delta y)^{2}}A^{\dagger}_{x}\text{ }+\cdots

\displaystyle\bra{u(x,y,t)}\text{ }\frac{1}{(\Delta y)^{4}}\text{ }-\bra{u(x,y,t)}\frac{2}{(\Delta y)^{2}}\times\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}}{(\Delta y)^{2}}-3\bra{u(x,y,t)}A_{x}-\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }3\bra{u(x,y,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}\text{ }-\frac{A^{\dagger}_{y}}{(\Delta y)^{2}}-\frac{2}{(\Delta y)^{2}}\text{ }-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{A_{y}}{(\Delta y)^{2}}+A^{\dagger}_{x}\frac{A^{\dagger}_{y}}{(\Delta y)^{2}}\text{ }+2\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}}{(\Delta y)^{2}}-A^{\dagger}_{x}\frac{A_{y}}{(\Delta y)^{2}}+\cdots

\displaystyle A_{x}\frac{A^{\dagger}_{y}}{(\Delta y)^{2}}-A_{x}\frac{2}{(\Delta y)^{2}}\big)\ket{u(x,y,t)}

\displaystyle 9\text{ }-\text{ }\big(\text{ }3\bra{u(x,y,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}-3\bra{u(x,y,t)}A_{x}-3\frac{A^{\dagger}_{y}}{(\Delta y)^{2}}-2+A_{y}+\cdots

\displaystyle\bra{u(x,y,t)}\text{ }A_{x}A_{x}-\bra{u(x,y,t)}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}-\bra{u(x,y,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}-\bra{u(x,y,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\text{ }-A^{\dagger}_{x}-\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle\frac{A^{\dagger}_{y}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}A_{x}\text{ }-\frac{A^{\dagger}_{y}}{(\Delta y)^{2}}A^{\dagger}_{x}\text{ }+\cdots

\displaystyle\bra{u(x,y,t)}\text{ }\frac{1}{(\Delta y)^{4}}\text{ }-\bra{u(x,y,t)}\frac{2}{(\Delta y)^{2}}\times\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}}{(\Delta y)^{2}}-3\bra{u(x,y,t)}A_{x}-\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }3\bra{u(x,y,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}\text{ }-\frac{A^{\dagger}_{y}}{(\Delta y)^{2}}-\frac{2}{(\Delta y)^{2}}\text{ }-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{A_{y}}{(\Delta y)^{2}}+A^{\dagger}_{x}\frac{A^{\dagger}_{y}}{(\Delta y)^{2}}\text{ }+2\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}}{(\Delta y)^{2}}-A^{\dagger}_{x}\frac{A_{y}}{(\Delta y)^{2}}+\cdots

\displaystyle A_{x}\frac{A^{\dagger}_{y}}{(\Delta y)^{2}}-A_{x}\frac{2}{(\Delta y)^{2}}\big)\ket{u(x,y,t)}\big)\text{ }\text{ }

\displaystyle\Updownarrow

\displaystyle\Big(\text{ }A_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\Big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-\text{ }2\Big)\ket{u(x,y,t)}\text{ }\Big)\text{ }\Big(\text{ }\bra{u(x,y,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}\big(\text{ }A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\text{ }\big)^{\dagger}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\text{ }\Big)\text{ }\text{ . }\text{ }

We continue rearranging terms from the superposition obtained in previous steps above,

\displaystyle\text{ }\Big(\text{ }A_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\Big(\text{ }A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\text{ }\Big)\text{ }\ket{u(x,y,t)}\bra{u(x,y,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}\text{ }\text{ }\text{ }\big(A_{x}-2\big)\text{ }\bra{u(x,y,t)}\text{ }\Big)\text{ }\text{ }

\displaystyle\Updownarrow\text{ }

\displaystyle\text{ }\Big(\text{ }A_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\Big(\text{ }A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}-2A^{\dagger}_{x}\Big)\text{ }\Big(\text{ }A_{x}-2\text{ }\Big)\bra{u(x,y,t)}\text{ }\Big)\text{ }

\displaystyle\text{ }\Updownarrow

\displaystyle\text{ }\Big(\text{ }A_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\Big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-\text{ }2A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+4A^{\dagger}_{x}\Big)\text{ }\bra{u(x,y,t)}\text{ }\Big)\text{ }

\displaystyle\text{ }\Updownarrow\text{ }

\displaystyle\Big(\text{ }A_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}\bra{u(x,y,t)}-2A_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\bra{u(x,y,t)}-2A_{x}\ket{u(t)}\text{ }\ket{u(t+\tau)}\text{ }+\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle 4\text{ }A_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\text{ }\Big)

\displaystyle 2\text{ }\big(\text{ }\bra{u(x,y,t)}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}-\text{ }\bra{u(x,y,t)}2A_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\times\cdots

\displaystyle\big(\text{ }3-\text{ }\bra{u(x,y,t)}A_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}-\bra{u(x,y,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\text{ . }

Collecting expectation values from the multiplication of quantum states yields,

\displaystyle 6\bra{u(x,y,t)}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\bra{u(x,y,t)}\text{ }A_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\text{ }-\cdots

\displaystyle 2\text{ }\bra{u(x,y,t)}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\bra{u(x,y,t)}\text{ }\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}-\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle\text{ }6\text{ }\bra{u(x,y,t)}2A_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\bra{u(x,y,t)}A_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}-\cdots

\displaystyle 2\bra{u(x,y,t)}\text{ }A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\text{ }\cdots

\displaystyle\bra{u(x,y,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}+\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle\text{ }2\bra{u(x,y,t)}2A_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle\bra{u(x,y,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\text{ }

\displaystyle\Updownarrow\text{ }

\displaystyle 6\bra{u(x,y,t)}A_{x}A_{x}\lambda^{2}A_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\text{ }-2\bra{u(x,y,t)}A_{x}A_{x}\lambda^{2}\text{ }A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\text{ }-\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle 6\bra{u(x,y,t)}2A_{x}\lambda^{2}\text{ }\ket{u(x,y,t)}\text{ }-2\bra{u(x,y,t)}\text{ }A_{x}A_{x}\lambda^{2}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\text{ }+\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle\text{ }2\bra{u(x,y,t)}2A_{x}\lambda^{2}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\text{ }\text{ . }

Previous rearrangements imply,

\displaystyle\big(6\bra{u(x,y,t)}A_{x}A_{x}\lambda^{2}A_{x}-2\bra{u(x,y,t)}A_{x}A_{x}\lambda^{2}A^{\dagger}_{x}-\cdots

\displaystyle 6\bra{u(x,y,t)}2A_{x}\lambda^{2}\text{ }-2\bra{u(x,y,t)}A_{x}A_{x}\lambda^{2}A^{\dagger}_{x}+2\bra{u(x,y,t)}2A_{x}\lambda^{2}A^{\dagger}_{x}+\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle\bra{u(x,y,t)}A_{x}A_{x}-\bra{u(x,y,t)}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}-\bra{u(x,y,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}-\bra{u(x,y,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}-A^{\dagger}_{x}-\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle\text{ }\frac{A^{\dagger}_{y}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}A_{x}-\frac{A^{\dagger}_{y}}{(\Delta y)^{2}}A^{\dagger}_{x}\text{ }+\bra{u(x,y,t)}\frac{1}{(\Delta y)^{4}\text{ }}-\bra{u(x,y,t)}\frac{2}{(\Delta y)^{2}}\text{ }\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}}{(\Delta y)^{2}}-\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }3\bra{u(x,y,t)}\text{ }A_{x}-3\bra{u(x,y,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}-\frac{A^{\dagger}_{y}}{(\Delta y)^{2}}\text{ }-\frac{2}{(\Delta y)^{2}}\text{ }-\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }\frac{A_{y}}{(\Delta y)^{2}}\text{ }+A^{\dagger}_{x}\frac{A^{\dagger}_{y}}{(\Delta y)^{2}}\text{ }+2\text{ }\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}}{(\Delta y)^{2}}-A^{\dagger}_{x}\frac{A_{y}}{(\Delta y)^{2}}\text{ }+\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle A_{x}\frac{A^{\dagger}_{y}}{(\Delta y)^{2}}-A_{x}\frac{2}{(\Delta y)^{2}}\text{ }\big)\ket{u(x,y,t)}\text{ }\text{ , }\text{ }

from which rearrangements of the final collection of terms yields,

\displaystyle\frac{\tau}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\Bigg\{A_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t+\tau)}A_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\big)\ket{u(x,y,t)}+\text{ }\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle A_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t+\tau)}-A_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t+\tau)}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}-A_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\big)+2\Bigg\}-\text{ }\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle\text{ }\frac{\tau}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\frac{A_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t+\tau)}}{(\Delta y)^{2}}\big(A^{\dagger}_{y}\ket{u(x,y,t)}-2\ket{u(x,y,t)}+\text{ }A_{y}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\big)\text{ }

\displaystyle\Updownarrow

\displaystyle\frac{\tau}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\Bigg\{\text{ }A_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t+\tau)}A_{x}\ket{u(x,y,\tau)}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}-\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle A_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t+\tau)}A_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}2\ket{u(x,y,t)}\text{ }\text{ }+\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }A_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t+\tau)}\text{ }-\text{ }A_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t+\tau)}\ket{u(x,y,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}-\text{ }\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }A_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t+\tau)}A_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\ket{u(x,y,t)}\bra{u(x,y,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}+2\Bigg\}\text{ }\text{ }-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{\tau}{(\Delta x)^{2}\text{ }(\Delta y)^{2}}\big(A_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t)}-\cdots

\displaystyle A_{x}\ket{u(x,y,t+\tau)}2\ket{u(x,y,t)}\big)\text{ . }

In conclusion, the cost function takes the form,

\displaystyle\boxed{\mathcal{C}^{L-T}(\lambda,\lambda^{\mathrm{L-T}}_{0})\text{ }}=\big(\lambda^{\mathrm{L-T}}_{0}\big)^{\text{ }2}+\text{ }\mathrm{Re}\text{ }\Bigg\{\text{ }\big(\frac{\tau}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\text{ }\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{\widetilde{u}}\text{ }-\text{ }2\text{ }\big(\text{ }\frac{\tau}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\text{ }\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }-\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }2\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }\frac{\tau}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bigg)^{2}\text{ }\bra{\widetilde{u}}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }+\text{ }6\text{ }\lambda^{2}\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }-2\text{ }\lambda^{2}\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\text{ }-\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle 4\text{ }\lambda^{2}\bra{u}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }+\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }+\bra{u}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }+\text{ }\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}+\text{ }\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }-\text{ }A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }+\text{ }\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }A^{\dagger}_{y}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }+\text{ }\frac{1}{(\Delta y)^{2}}\text{ }A^{\dagger}_{y}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }+\text{ }\frac{1}{(\Delta y)^{4}}\text{ }\bra{u}\ket{u}\text{ }+\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle\text{ }\frac{2}{(\Delta y)^{4}}\text{ }A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }+\text{ }3\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }+\text{ }3\text{ }\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }+\text{ }\frac{1}{(\Delta y)^{2}}\text{ }A^{\dagger}_{y}\ket{u}\text{ }+2\text{ }\ket{u}\text{ }+\text{ }\frac{1}{(\Delta y)^{2}}\text{ }A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }+\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle\text{ }3\text{ }\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }+\text{ }\frac{1}{(\Delta y)^{2}}\text{ }A^{\dagger}_{y}\ket{u}\text{ }-\text{ }\frac{2}{(\Delta y)^{2}}\ket{u}\text{ }+\text{ }\frac{1}{(\Delta y)^{2}}A_{y}\ket{u}\text{ }+\text{ }\frac{1}{(\Delta y)^{2}}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{y}\ket{u}\text{ }+\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }2\text{ }\frac{1}{(\Delta y)^{2}}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }+\text{ }\frac{1}{(\Delta y)^{2}}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{y}\ket{u}\text{ }+\text{ }\frac{1}{(\Delta y)^{2}}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{y}\ket{u}\text{ }+\text{ }2\text{ }\frac{1}{(\Delta y)^{2}}A_{x}\ket{u}+\text{ }\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }-\text{ }\frac{2\tau\text{ }}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }+\frac{\tau}{(\Delta x)^{2}(\Delta y)^{2}}\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }+\text{ }\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }+\text{ }2\frac{\tau}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }+\frac{\tau}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\bra{\widetilde{u}}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{\widetilde{u}}\text{ }\Bigg\}\text{ }\text{ . }

4.8
Camassa-Holm equation

Cost function derivation

For
\kappa\equiv 1
, the equation after introducing the standard quantum state transformation for evolution of the variational state for the solution is equivalent to,

\displaystyle u_{t}=2u_{x}u_{xx}+uu_{xxx}-(3u+2\kappa)u_{x}+u_{xxt}

\displaystyle\text{ }\Updownarrow\text{ }

\displaystyle\frac{\ket{u(x,t+\tau)}-\ket{u(x,t)}}{\tau}=2\big(\frac{A_{x}-\textbf{1}}{\Delta x}\big)\text{ }\ket{u(x,t)}\big(\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)\times\cdots

\displaystyle\ket{u(x,t)}+\frac{\ket{u(x,t)}}{\Delta x}\times\cdots

\displaystyle\bigg[\big(\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)\ket{u(x,t+\tau)}-\cdots

\displaystyle\big(\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)\ket{u(x,t)}\bigg]-\big(3\ket{u(x,t)}+2\big)\text{ }\big(\frac{A_{x}-\textbf{1}}{\Delta x}\big)\text{ }\ket{u(x,t)}+\big(\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\ket{u(x,t)}\big)_{t}\text{ . }

We evaluate the first order time derivative and additional terms of the superposition,

\displaystyle\frac{\big(\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)\text{ }\ket{u(x,t+\tau)}-\big(\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)\ket{u(x,t)}}{(\Delta x)\tau}

\displaystyle\text{ }\Updownarrow\text{ }

\displaystyle\big(3\ket{u(x,t)}+2\big)\big(\frac{A_{x}-\textbf{1}}{\Delta x}\big)\text{ }\ket{u(x,t)}=\frac{1}{\Delta x}\text{ }\big(3\ket{u(x,t)}+2\big)\times\cdots

\displaystyle\big(A_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}-\ket{u(x,t)}\big)=\frac{1}{\Delta x}\text{ }\big(\{3\ket{u(x,t)}\}\cdots

\displaystyle\{A_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}\}-\{3\ket{u(x,t)}\}\{\ket{u(x,t)}\}+2A_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}-2\ket{u(x,t)}\big)

\displaystyle\text{ }\Updownarrow\text{ }

\displaystyle\big(\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)\text{ }\ket{u(x,t)}=\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}-2\ket{u(x,t)}+A_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}\big)

\displaystyle\text{ }\Updownarrow\text{ }

\displaystyle\text{ }\big(\text{ }\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)\text{ }\ket{u(x,t+\tau)}=\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,t+\tau)}-\cdots

\displaystyle 2\ket{u(x,t+\tau)}+A_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}\big)

\displaystyle\text{ }\Updownarrow\text{ }

\displaystyle\big(\frac{A_{x}-\textbf{1}}{\Delta x}\big)\text{ }\ket{u(x,t)}\text{ }\big(\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)\ket{u(x,t)}

\displaystyle\text{ }\Updownarrow

\displaystyle\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\bigg[\{A_{x}-\textbf{1}\}\ket{u(x,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\{A_{x}-\textbf{1}\}\ket{u(x,t)}+\{A_{x}-\text{ }\textbf{1}\}\ket{u(x,t)}A_{x}\bigg]\ket{u(x,t)}

for terms on the right hand side of the equation, and,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\bigg[A_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}-\ket{u(x,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}-2A_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}-\ket{u(x,t)}+A_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}A_{x}-\ket{u(x,t)}A_{x}\bigg]\ket{u(x,t)}\text{ }

\displaystyle\Updownarrow

\displaystyle\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\bigg[A_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}-\ket{u(x,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}-2A_{x}\bigg]\text{ , }

for terms on the left hand side of the equation. As a consequence of previous manipulations, the time evolved variational state corresponding to the solution takes the form,

\displaystyle\ket{u(x,t+\tau)}=\tau\text{ }\bigg\{\text{ }\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\big(A_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}-\ket{u(x,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}-2A_{x}\big)+\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{\ket{u(x,t)}}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\text{ }\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,t+\tau)}-2\ket{u(x,t+\tau)}+A_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}\big)-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{\ket{u(x,t)}}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\bigg(A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}-2\ket{u(x,t)}+A_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}\bigg)-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\Delta x}\big({3\ket{u(x,t)}}{A_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}}-{3\ket{u(x,t)}}{\ket{u(x,t)}}+2A_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}-2\ket{u(x,t)}\big)+\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{\big(\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)\text{ }\ket{u(x,t+\tau)}-\big(\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)\text{ }\ket{u(x,t)}}{(\Delta x)\tau}\bigg\}

In turn, the superposition above yields the cost function that can be identified with the expansion of the norm below, after consolidating terms with the time evolved variational state of the solution,

\displaystyle\big(\text{ }1+\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{3}\tau}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\ket{u(x,t+\tau)}=\tau\text{ }\bigg\{\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\text{ }\big(A_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}-\ket{u(x,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}-2A_{x}\big)+\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{\ket{u(x,t)}}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\bigg(A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,t+\tau)}-2\ket{u(x,t+\tau)}+A_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}\bigg)-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{\ket{u(x,t)}}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\text{ }\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}-2\ket{u(x,t)}+A_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}\big)-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\Delta x}\bigg({3\ket{u(x,t)}}{A_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}}-{3\ket{u(x,t)}}{\ket{u(x,t)}}+2A_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}-2\ket{u(x,t)}\bigg)+\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{\big(\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)\ket{u(x,t+\tau)}-\big(\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)\ket{u(x,t)}}{(\Delta x)\tau}\text{ }\bigg\}

\displaystyle\Updownarrow\text{ }

\displaystyle\text{ }\ket{u(x,t+\tau)}=\frac{\tau}{\bigg(\text{ }1+\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{3}\tau}\text{ }\bigg)}\bigg\{\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\bigg(A_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}-\ket{u(x,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}-2A_{x}\bigg)+\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{\ket{u(x,t)}}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\bigg(A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,t+\tau)}-2\ket{u(x,t+\tau)}+A_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}\bigg)-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{\ket{u(x,t)}}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\bigg(A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}-2\ket{u(x,t)}+A_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}\bigg)-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\Delta x}\bigg({3\ket{u(x,t)}}{A_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}}-{3\ket{u(x,t)}}{\ket{u(x,t)}}+2A_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}-2\ket{u(x,t)}\bigg)+\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{\big(\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)\ket{u(x,t+\tau)}-\big(\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)\text{ }\ket{u(x,t)}}{(\Delta x)\tau}\bigg\}\text{ . }

Expanding the square of the following norm yields,

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}^{\text{ }\mathrm{C-H}}=\bigg|\bigg|\ket{u(x,t+\tau)}-\ket{u(x,t)}\bigg|\bigg|^{2}

\displaystyle\Updownarrow

\displaystyle\bigg|\bigg|\frac{\tau}{\big(\text{ }1+\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{3}\tau}\text{ }\big)}\bigg\{\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\big(A_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}-\ket{u(x,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}-2A_{x}\big)+\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{\ket{u(x,t)}}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\bigg(A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,t+\tau)}-2\ket{u(x,t+\tau)}+A_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}\bigg)-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{\ket{u(x,t)}}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\bigg(A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}-2\ket{u(x,t)}+A_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}\bigg)-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\Delta x}\bigg({3\ket{u(x,t)}}{A_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}}-{3\ket{u(x,t)}}{\ket{u(x,t)}}+2A_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}-2\ket{u(x,t)}\bigg)+\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{\big(\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)\ket{u(x,t+\tau)}-\big(\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)\ket{u(x,t)}}{(\Delta x)\tau}\bigg\}-\ket{u(x,t)}\bigg|\bigg|^{2}\text{ . }

As with derivations of previous cost functions, the first component of the superposition is solely dependent upon the initial choice of variational parameter
\lambda
, while remaining terms are inversely proportional with respect to either the time step
\tau
with which the solution state is evolved, or the grid resolution
\Delta x
. The superposition is of the form,

\displaystyle\lambda^{2}+\bigg\{\frac{\tau}{\big(\text{ }1+\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{3}\tau}\text{ }\big)}\bigg\{\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\big(A_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}-\ket{u(x,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}-2A_{x}\big)+\cdots

\displaystyle 2\lambda^{2}\frac{\tau}{\big(\text{ }1+\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{3}\tau}\text{ }\big)}\bigg\{\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\big(A_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}-\ket{u(x,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}-2A_{x}\big)+\cdots

Expanding all terms in the order that they appear in the superposition following
\lambda^{2}

We square the terms included in the superposition above to obtain,

\displaystyle\text{ }\bigg\{\frac{\tau}{\big(\text{ }1+\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{3}\tau}\text{ }\big)}\bigg\{\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\big(A_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}-\ket{u(x,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}-2A_{x}\big)+\cdots

Furthermore, following necessary rearrangements of the expansion provides,

\displaystyle\text{ }\bigg\{\frac{\tau}{\big(\text{ }1+\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{3}\tau}\text{ }\big)}\bigg\{\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\bigg(A_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}-\ket{u(x,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}-2A_{x}\bigg)+\cdots

Distributing further,

\displaystyle\frac{\tau^{2}}{\big(\text{ }1+\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{3}\tau}\text{ }\big)^{2}}\bigg\{\text{ }\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\bigg(A_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}-\ket{u(x,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}-2A_{x}\bigg)+\cdots

From the resulting terms above, the terms of the superposition following the prefactor dependent upon

is,

\displaystyle\frac{\tau^{2}}{\big(\text{ }1+\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{3}\tau}\text{ }\big)^{2}}\bigg\{\text{ }\bigg(\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\bigg(A_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}-\ket{u(x,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}-2A_{x}\bigg)+\cdots

\displaystyle 2\bigg(\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\bigg(A_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}-\ket{u(x,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}-2A_{x}\bigg)\text{ }+\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle\frac{\tau^{2}}{\Delta x\big(\text{ }1+\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{3}\tau}\text{ }\big)^{2}}\bigg(\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }3\ket{u(x,t)}A_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}-\text{ }3\text{ }\ket{u(x,t)}A_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}\bigg)^{2}\text{ }-\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle\text{ }\frac{\tau^{2}}{\big(\text{ }1+\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{3}\tau}\text{ }\big)^{2}}\bigg\{\text{ }\bigg(\frac{4}{(\Delta x)^{6}}\bigg(A_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}-\ket{u(x,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}-2A_{x}\bigg)^{2}+\cdots

corresponding to terms of the superposition from squaring expectation values, as shown above. The remaining terms are,

\displaystyle\frac{8}{(\Delta x)^{9}}\text{ }\bigg(A_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}-\ket{u(x,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}-2A_{x}\bigg)\text{ }\times\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle 6\bra{u(x,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}\bra{u(x,t)}\ket{u(x,t)}A_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}\text{ }+\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{\tau^{2}}{\big(\text{ }1+\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{3}\tau}\text{ }\big)^{2}}\bigg\{\text{ }\frac{4}{(\Delta x)^{6}}\bigg(\bra{u(x,t)}A_{x}\bra{u(x,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}-\cdots

while the cross term resulting in the superposition with prefactor

\displaystyle\frac{2\tau^{2}}{\Delta x\big(\text{ }1+\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{3}\tau}\text{ }\big)^{2}}\bigg(6\ket{u(x,t)}A_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}A_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}-\cdots

\displaystyle 6\bra{u(x,t)}\ket{u(x,t)}A_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}-\cdots

\displaystyle 6\ket{u(x,t)}A_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}A_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}+\cdots

\displaystyle 6\bra{u(x,t)}\ket{u(x,t)}A_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}\bigg)\text{ . }

Overall, the superposition with prefactor
\frac{\tau^{2}}{\Delta x\big(1+\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{2}\tau}\big)^{2}}
becomes,

\displaystyle\frac{\tau^{2}}{\Delta x\big(1+\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{2}\tau}\big)^{2}}\bigg(\text{ }\big(\text{ }\bra{u(x,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}\bra{u(x,t)}9\ket{u(x,t)}A_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}-\cdots

\displaystyle 6\bra{u(x,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}\bra{u(x,t)}\ket{u(x,t)}A_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}\text{ }+\cdots

\displaystyle 9\bra{u(x,t)}A^{\dagger}_{x}\bra{u(x,t)}\ket{u(x,t)}A_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }-\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle 2\big(\text{ }6\ket{u(x,t)}A_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}A_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}-6\bra{u(x,t)}\ket{u(x,t)}A_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}-\cdots

\displaystyle 6\ket{u(x,t)}A_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}A_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}+\cdots

\displaystyle 6\bra{u(x,t)}\ket{u(x,t)}A_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\text{ }+\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }\bra{u(x,t)}4A_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}-\bra{u(x,t)}8A_{x}\ket{u(x,t)}\text{ }+\bra{u(x,t)}8\ket{u(x,t)}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }\text{ , }

implying that the finalized cost function the quantum state whose ground state we approximate is,

\displaystyle\boxed{\mathcal{C}^{\text{ }\mathrm{C-H}}(\lambda,\lambda^{\text{ }\mathrm{C-H}}_{0})\text{ }}=\big(\lambda^{\text{ }\mathrm{C-H}}_{0}\big)^{2}\text{ }+\text{ }\mathrm{Re}\text{ }\bigg\{\text{ }\frac{4\lambda^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\frac{\tau}{\bigg(\text{ }1+\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{3}\tau}\bigg)}\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }-\text{ }\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle\text{ }\frac{2\lambda^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\frac{\tau}{\bigg(\text{ }1+\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{3}\tau}\bigg)}\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}-\text{ }\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }\frac{8\lambda^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\frac{\tau}{\bigg(\text{ }1+\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{3}\tau}\bigg)}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }+\text{ }\frac{4\lambda^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\frac{\tau}{\bigg(\text{ }1+\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{3}\tau}\bigg)}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{\widetilde{u}}\text{ }+\text{ }\frac{8\lambda^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{6}}\frac{\tau}{\bigg(\text{ }1+\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{3}\tau}\bigg)}\ket{\widetilde{u}}\text{ }+\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }\frac{8\lambda^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{6}}\frac{\tau}{\bigg(\text{ }1+\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{3}\tau}\bigg)}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }+\text{ }\frac{8\lambda^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{6}}\frac{\tau}{\bigg(\text{ }1+\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{3}\tau}\bigg)}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }-\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }\frac{4\lambda^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{6}}\frac{\tau}{\bigg(\text{ }1+\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{3}\tau}\bigg)}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\text{ }-\text{ }\frac{8\lambda^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{6}}\frac{\tau}{\bigg(\text{ }1+\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{3}\tau}\bigg)}\ket{\widetilde{u}}\text{ }-\text{ }\frac{4\lambda^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{6}}\frac{\tau}{\bigg(\text{ }1+\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{3}\tau}\bigg)}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }-\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }\frac{4\lambda^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{6}}\frac{\tau}{\bigg(\text{ }1+\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{3}\tau}\bigg)}\text{ }-\text{ }\bigg(\frac{3}{\Delta x}\bigg)\text{ }\frac{4\lambda^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\frac{\tau}{\bigg(\text{ }1+\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{3}\tau}\bigg)}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }+\text{ }\frac{12\lambda^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\frac{\tau}{\bigg(\text{ }1+\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{3}\tau}\bigg)}\bra{u}\ket{u}\text{ }+\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }\frac{8\lambda^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\frac{\tau}{\bigg(\text{ }1+\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{3}\tau}\bigg)}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }+\text{ }\frac{4\lambda^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\frac{\tau}{\bigg(\text{ }1+\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{3}\tau}\bigg)}\ket{u}\text{ }+\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }\frac{4\lambda^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }\bigg(\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }\frac{\tau}{\bigg(\text{ }1+\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{3}\tau}\bigg)}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{\widetilde{u}}\text{ }-\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }\frac{4\lambda^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{5}}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }\bigg(\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }\frac{\tau}{\bigg(\text{ }1+\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{3}\tau}\bigg)}\ket{\widetilde{u}}\text{ }+\text{ }2\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }\frac{4\lambda^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }\bigg(\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }\frac{\tau}{\bigg(\text{ }1+\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{3}\tau}\bigg)}A_{x}\ket{\widetilde{u}}-\text{ }\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }\frac{4\lambda^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }\bigg(\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }\frac{\tau}{\bigg(\text{ }1+\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{3}\tau}\bigg)}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }+\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }\frac{4\lambda^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }\bigg(\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }\frac{\tau}{\bigg(\text{ }1+\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{3}\tau}\bigg)}\ket{u}\text{ }-\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }\frac{4\lambda^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }\bigg(\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }\frac{\tau}{\bigg(\text{ }1+\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{3}\tau}\bigg)}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }+\text{ }\frac{8\tau^{2}}{\bigg(1+\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{3}\tau}\bigg)^{2}}\bigg(\text{ }\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{6}}\text{ }\bigg)\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }-\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }\frac{8\tau^{2}}{\bigg(1+\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{3}\tau}\bigg)^{2}}\bigg(\text{ }\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{6}}\text{ }\bigg)\bigg(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\bigg)\text{ }\bigg(\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}\bigg)\text{ }+\text{ }\frac{4\tau^{2}}{\bigg(1+\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{3}\tau}\bigg)^{2}}\bigg(\text{ }\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{6}}\text{ }\bigg)\times\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }\bra{u}\ket{u}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }+\text{ }\frac{4\tau^{2}}{\bigg(1+\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{3}\tau}\bigg)^{2}}\text{ }\bigg(\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{6}}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }\bigg(\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{\widetilde{u}}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }\bra{\widetilde{u}}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{4\tau^{2}}{\bigg(1+\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{3}\tau}\bigg)^{2}}\text{ }\bigg(\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{6}}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }\bra{u}\ket{\widetilde{u}}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }\bra{\widetilde{u}}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }+\text{ }\frac{4\tau^{2}}{\bigg(1+\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{3}\tau}\bigg)^{2}}\text{ }\bigg(\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{6}}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }\times\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }\bra{\widetilde{u}}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }+\text{ }\frac{4\tau^{2}}{\bigg(1+\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{3}\tau}\bigg)^{2}}\text{ }\bigg(\frac{8}{(\Delta x)^{9}}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{\widetilde{u}}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }-\text{ }\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }\frac{4\tau^{2}}{\bigg(1+\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{3}\tau}\bigg)^{2}}\text{ }\bigg(\frac{16}{(\Delta x)^{9}}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }\bra{u}\ket{\widetilde{u}}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }+\text{ }\frac{4\tau^{2}}{\bigg(1+\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{3}\tau}\bigg)^{2}}\text{ }\bigg(\frac{8}{(\Delta x)^{9}}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }-\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }\frac{4\tau^{2}}{\bigg(1+\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{3}\tau}\bigg)^{2}}\text{ }\bigg(\frac{8}{(\Delta x)^{9}}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{\widetilde{u}}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }-\frac{4\tau^{2}}{\bigg(1+\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{3}\tau}\bigg)^{2}}\text{ }\bigg(\frac{16}{(\Delta x)^{9}}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }\bra{u}\ket{\widetilde{u}}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }\times\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }-\text{ }\frac{4\tau^{2}}{\bigg(1+\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{3}\tau}\bigg)^{2}}\text{ }\bigg(\frac{8}{(\Delta x)^{9}}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }+\frac{\tau^{2}}{\bigg(1+\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{3}\tau}\bigg)^{2}}\text{ }\times\cdots

\displaystyle\bigg(\text{ }\frac{4}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-\text{ }\frac{\tau^{2}}{\bigg(1+\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{3}\tau}\bigg)^{2}}\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }\frac{4}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }-\text{ }\frac{\tau^{2}}{\bigg(1+\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{3}\tau}\bigg)^{2}}\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }\frac{8}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }A_{x}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }\frac{2\tau^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{3}\bigg(1+\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{3}\tau}\bigg)^{2}}\text{ }\bra{\widetilde{u}}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }+\text{ }\frac{4\tau^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{3}\bigg(1+\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{3}\tau}\bigg)^{2}}\text{ }\bra{\widetilde{u}}\ket{u}\text{ }+\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }\frac{2\tau^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{3}\bigg(1+\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{3}\tau}\bigg)^{2}}\text{ }\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }+\text{ }\frac{2\tau^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{3}\text{ }\bigg(1+\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{3}\tau}\bigg)^{2}}\text{ }\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }+\frac{9\tau^{2}}{\Delta x\text{ }\bigg(1+\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{2}\tau}\bigg)^{2}}\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }\bra{u}\ket{u}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }\times\cdots

\displaystyle\bigg(\text{ }\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\bigg)-\text{ }\frac{6\tau^{2}}{\Delta x\text{ }\bigg(1+\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{3}\tau}\bigg)^{2}}\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }\bra{u}\ket{u}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }-\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }\frac{9\tau^{2}}{\Delta x\text{ }\bigg(1+\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{3}\tau}\bigg)^{2}}\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }\bra{u}\ket{u}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }-12\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }-12\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }\bra{u}\ket{u}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }A_{x}\ket{u}+\text{ }\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }12\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }\bra{u}\ket{u}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }+\text{ }4\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }-\text{ }8\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }+\text{ }8\bra{u}\ket{u}\bigg\}\text{ }\text{ . }

4.9
Drinfeld-Sokolov-Wilson equations

Cost function derivation

In comparison to previous cost functions, one can also provide multiple cost functions for solving PDE systems. One such system includes,

\displaystyle\frac{\partial u}{\partial t}+3v\frac{\partial v}{\partial x}=0\text{ , }

\displaystyle\frac{\partial v}{\partial t}=2\frac{\partial^{3}v}{\partial x^{3}}+\frac{\partial u}{\partial x}+2u\frac{\partial v}{\partial x}\text{ . }

Along the lines of previous arrangements, we obtain expressions for the time evolved variational states of the solutions
u,v
to the system, each of which are individually of the form,

\displaystyle\frac{\ket{u(t+\tau)}-\ket{u}}{t}+3\ket{v}\text{ }\big(\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-\textbf{1}}{\Delta x}\big)\text{ }\ket{v}=0

\displaystyle\Updownarrow

\displaystyle\ket{u(t+\tau)}=-3tv\text{ }\big(\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-\textbf{1}}{\Delta x}\big)\text{ }\ket{v}+\ket{v}

\displaystyle\Updownarrow

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}^{\mathrm{DSW}}_{u}(\lambda,\lambda_{0})=\bigg|\bigg|\ket{u(t+\tau)}-\ket{u(t)}\bigg|\bigg|^{2}=\bigg|\bigg|\text{ }\big(-3t\ket{v}\text{ }\big(\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-\textbf{1}}{\Delta x}\text{ }\big)\ket{v}+\ket{v}\big)-\ket{u(t)}\bigg|\bigg|^{2}

\displaystyle\Updownarrow

\displaystyle\big(-3t\ket{v}\big(\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-\textbf{1}}{\Delta x}\big)\ket{v}+\ket{v}\big)^{2}-2\big(-3t\ket{v}\big(\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-\textbf{1}}{\Delta x}\big)\ket{v}+\ket{v}\big)\ket{u(t)}+\lambda^{2}

\displaystyle\Updownarrow

\displaystyle\big(\text{ }\big\{-3t\ket{v}\big(\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-\textbf{1}}{\Delta x}\big)\ket{v}\big\}^{2}-6t\ket{v}\big(\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-\textbf{1}}{\Delta x}\big)\ket{v}+\lambda^{2}\big)\ket{u(t)}+\lambda^{2}

\displaystyle\Updownarrow

\displaystyle\big(\text{ }9t^{2}\ket{v}\text{ }\frac{\big(A_{x}-\textbf{1}\big)}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\lambda^{2}-6t\ket{v}\text{ }\big(\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-\textbf{1}}{\Delta x}\big)\text{ }\ket{v}+\lambda^{2}\big)\text{ }\ket{u(t)}+\lambda^{2}

\displaystyle\Updownarrow

\displaystyle\boxed{\mathcal{C}^{DSW}_{u}(\lambda,\lambda^{u}_{0})}=\text{ }\frac{9t^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big(\lambda^{u}_{0}\big)^{2}\text{ }\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{v}\text{ }-\text{ }\frac{9t^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big(\lambda^{u}_{0}\big)^{2}\ket{u}\text{ }-\text{ }\frac{6t\ket{v}}{\Delta x}\bra{v}A_{x}\ket{u}+\big(\lambda^{u}_{0}\big)^{2}\text{ }\ket{u}+\text{ }\big({\lambda^{u}_{0}}\big)^{2}\text{ }\text{ , }

for the first equation of the system, and,

\displaystyle\frac{\ket{v(t+\tau)}-\ket{v}}{t}=2\bigg\{\frac{\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\ket{v(t+\tau)}-\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\ket{v}}{\Delta x}\bigg\}+\big(\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-\textbf{1}}{\Delta x}\big)\ket{u}+2\ket{u}\big(\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-\textbf{1}}{\Delta x}\big)\text{ }\ket{v}

\displaystyle\Updownarrow

\displaystyle\ket{v(t+\tau)}=\tau\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }2\bigg\{\frac{\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\ket{v(t+\tau)}-\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\ket{v}}{\Delta x}\bigg\}+\big(\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-\textbf{1}}{\Delta x}\big)\ket{u}+2\ket{u}\big(\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-\textbf{1}}{\Delta x}\big)\ket{v}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }+\ket{v}

\displaystyle\Updownarrow

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}^{DSW}_{v}(\lambda,\lambda_{0})=\bigg|\bigg|\ket{v(t+\tau)}-\ket{v}\bigg|\bigg|^{2}

\displaystyle\Updownarrow

\displaystyle\bigg|\bigg|\tau\text{ }\big(\text{ }2\bigg\{\frac{\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\ket{v(t+\tau)}-\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\ket{v}}{\Delta x}\bigg\}+\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-\textbf{1}\big)\ket{u}+2\ket{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-\textbf{1}\big)\text{ }\ket{v}\text{ }\big)\text{ }+\ket{v}-\ket{v}\bigg|\bigg|^{2}

\displaystyle\Updownarrow

\displaystyle\bigg|\bigg|2\frac{\tau}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\bigg\{\text{ }\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\text{ }\ket{v(t+\tau)}-\text{ }\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\text{ }\ket{v}\text{ }\bigg\}+A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle 2\ket{v}\text{ }\big(\text{ }A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{v}-\ket{v}\text{ }\big)\bigg|\bigg|^{2}

\displaystyle\Updownarrow

\displaystyle\text{ }\big(\text{ }2\frac{\tau}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\bigg\{\text{ }\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\bigg)\text{ }\ket{v(t+\tau)}-\text{ }\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\text{ }\ket{v}\text{ }\bigg\}+A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\ket{u}\text{ }\big)^{2}+\cdots

\displaystyle\big(\text{ }4\frac{\tau}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\bigg\{\text{ }\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\text{ }\ket{v(t+\tau)}-\text{ }\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\text{ }\ket{v}\text{ }\bigg\}+A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\ket{u}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\times\cdots

\displaystyle\big(\text{ }\bra{v}A_{x}\ket{v}+\lambda^{2}\text{ }\big)+\big(\text{ }2\ket{v}\big(\text{ }A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{v}-\ket{v}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\big)^{2}\text{ , }

for the second equation of the system, from which expanding the first term in the wavefunction yields,

\displaystyle\big(\text{ }2\frac{\tau}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\bigg\{\text{ }\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\bigg)\text{ }\ket{v(t+\tau)}-\text{ }\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\text{ }\ket{v}\text{ }\bigg\}+A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\ket{u}\text{ }\big)^{2}

\displaystyle\Updownarrow

\displaystyle\big(\text{ }2\frac{\tau}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\bigg\{\text{ }\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\ket{v(t+\tau)}-\text{ }\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\text{ }\ket{v}\text{ }\bigg\}\text{ }\big)^{2}+\cdots

\displaystyle 4\frac{\tau}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\bigg\{\text{ }\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\text{ }\ket{v(t+\tau)}-\text{ }\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\text{ }\ket{v}\text{ }\bigg\}\text{ }\big(\text{ }A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\ket{u}\text{ }\big)+\cdots

\displaystyle\big(\text{ }A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\ket{u}\text{ }\big)^{2}

\displaystyle\Updownarrow

\displaystyle\big(\text{ }2\frac{\tau}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\text{ }\big)^{2}\text{ }\bigg\{\text{ }\big(\text{ }\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\text{ }\ket{v(t+\tau)}\big)^{2}-2\text{ }\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\text{ }\ket{v(t+\tau)}\text{ }\times\cdots

\displaystyle\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\text{ }\ket{v}+\bigg(\text{ }\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\ket{v}\text{ }\bigg)^{2}\bigg\}+\cdots

\displaystyle 4\frac{\tau}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\bigg\{\text{ }\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\text{ }\ket{v(t+\tau)}-\text{ }\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\text{ }\ket{v}\text{ }\bigg\}\text{ }\big(\text{ }A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\ket{u}\text{ }\big)+\cdots

\displaystyle\big(\text{ }A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\ket{u}\text{ }\big)^{2}

\displaystyle\Updownarrow

\displaystyle\big(\text{ }2\frac{\tau}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\text{ }\big)^{2}\bigg\{\text{ }\big(\text{ }\big(\text{ }A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\text{ }\ket{v(t+\tau)}\big)\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\text{ }\ket{v(t+\tau)}\bigg)-\cdots

\displaystyle 2\text{ }\bigg(A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{v(t+\tau)}-2\ket{v(t+\tau)}+A_{x}\ket{v(t+\tau)}\bigg)\text{ }\bigg(A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{v}-2\ket{v}+A_{x}\ket{v}\bigg)\text{ }+\cdots

\displaystyle\bigg(\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\ket{v}\bigg)\bigg(\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\text{ }\big)\ket{v}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }\bigg\}\text{ , }

corresponding to the second equation of the system. We proceed,

\displaystyle\big(\text{ }2\frac{\tau}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\text{ }\big)^{2}\bigg\{\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }\bigg(A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{v(t+\tau)}-2\ket{v(t+\tau)}+A_{x}\ket{v(t+\tau)}\bigg)\text{ }\bigg)\times\cdots

\displaystyle\bigg(\text{ }\bigg(A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{v(t+\tau)}-2\ket{v(t+\tau)}+A_{x}\ket{v(t+\tau)}\bigg)\text{ }\bigg)-\cdots

\displaystyle 2\text{ }\bigg(A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{v(t+\tau)}-2\ket{v(t+\tau)}+A_{x}\ket{v(t+\tau)}\bigg)\text{ }\bigg(A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{v}-2\ket{v}+A_{x}\ket{v}\bigg)\text{ }+\cdots

\displaystyle\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{v}-2\ket{v}+A_{x}\ket{v}\big)\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{v}-2\ket{v}+A_{x}\ket{v}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\bigg\}

\displaystyle\Updownarrow

\displaystyle\big(\text{ }2\frac{\tau}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\text{ }\big)^{2}\bigg\{\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }\bra{v(t+\tau)}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{v(t+\tau)}-\bra{v(t+\tau)}2A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{v(t+\tau)}+\cdots

\displaystyle\bra{v(t+\tau)}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{v(t+\tau)}\text{ }-\bra{v(t+\tau)}2A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{v(t+\tau)}-\bra{v(t+\tau)}4\ket{v(t+\tau)}-\cdots

\displaystyle\bra{v(t+\tau)}2A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{v(t+\tau)}+\bra{v(t+\tau}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{v(t+\tau)}-\bra{v(t+\tau)}2A_{x}\ket{v(t+\tau)}+\cdots

\displaystyle\bra{v(t+\tau)}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{v(t+\tau)}\bigg)-\cdots

\displaystyle 2\text{ }\bigg(\bra{v}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{v(t+\tau)}-\bra{v}2A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{v(t+\tau)}-\bra{v}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{v(t+\tau)}-\cdots

\displaystyle\bra{v}2A_{x}\ket{v(t+\tau)}-\bra{v}4\ket{v(t+\tau)}-\bra{v}2A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{v(t+\tau)}+\cdots

\displaystyle\bra{v}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{v(t+\tau)}-\bra{v}2A_{x}\ket{v(t+\tau)}+\bra{v}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{v(t+\tau)}\bigg)\text{ }+\cdots

\displaystyle\big(\text{ }\bra{v}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{v}-\bra{v}2A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{v}+\bra{v}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{v}-\bra{v}2A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{v}-4\bra{v}\ket{v}-\bra{v}2A_{x}\ket{v}-\bra{v}2\ket{v}-\cdots

\displaystyle\bra{v}2A_{x}\ket{v}+\bra{v}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{v}-\bra{v}2A_{x}\ket{v}+\bra{v}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{v}\big)\text{ }\bigg\}\text{ }\text{ . }

We expand the square of the last term of the superposition,

\displaystyle\big(\text{ }A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\ket{u}\text{ }\big)^{2}=\big(\text{ }A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\big)^{2}+2A_{x}\ket{u}\bra{u}+\lambda^{2}=\bra{u}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+2A_{x}\ket{u}\bra{u}+\lambda^{2}\text{ , }

from which we rearrange terms from the square of the expansion of the last term immediately preceding the expansion of the norm,

\displaystyle\big(\text{ }2\ket{v}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{v}-\ket{v}\big)\text{ }\big)\text{ }\big(2\ket{v}\text{ }\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{v}-\ket{v}\big)\text{ }\big)=\big(2\ket{v}\text{ }\big(\text{ }A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{v}\big(\text{ }2\ket{v}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{v}-\ket{v}\text{ }\big)-\cdots

\displaystyle 2\ket{v}\bra{v}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{v}-\ket{v}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\big)\text{ }\big)\text{ }\big)\text{ . }

With the terms from the previous expansion, we collect terms from the second term in the expansion of the norm,

\displaystyle\big(\text{ }4\frac{\tau}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\bigg\{\text{ }\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\text{ }\ket{v(t+\tau)}-\text{ }\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\text{ }\ket{v}\text{ }\bigg\}+A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\ket{u}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\times\cdots

\displaystyle\big(\bra{v}A_{x}\ket{v}+\lambda^{2}\big)

\displaystyle\Updownarrow

\displaystyle\big(\text{ }4\frac{\tau}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\bigg\{\text{ }\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\text{ }\ket{v(t+\tau)}-\text{ }\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\text{ }\ket{v}\text{ }\bigg\}+A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\ket{u}\text{ }\big)\times\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }\big(\bra{v}A_{x}\ket{v}\big)+\big(\text{ }4\lambda^{2}\frac{\tau}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\bigg\{\text{ }\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\text{ }\ket{v(t+\tau)}-\text{ }\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\text{ }\ket{v}\text{ }\bigg\}+A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\ket{u}\text{ }\big)\text{ , }

from which substituting in the expansion of the first and third terms of the previous superposition yields all expectation values with prefactor
4\frac{\tau}{(\Delta x)^{2}}
,

\displaystyle\bigg(\text{ }4\frac{\tau}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\bigg\{\text{ }\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\text{ }\ket{v(t+\tau)}\text{ }\big(\bra{v}A_{x}\ket{v}\big)-\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\text{ }\ket{v}\text{ }\big(\bra{v}A_{x}\ket{v}\big)\text{ }\bigg\}+\cdots

\displaystyle A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\big(\bra{v}A_{x}\ket{v}\big)-\ket{u}\text{ }\big(\bra{v}A_{x}\ket{v}\big)\text{ }\bigg)+\cdots

\displaystyle\bigg(\text{ }4\lambda^{2}\frac{\tau}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\bigg\{\text{ }\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\text{ }\ket{v(t+\tau)}-\text{ }\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\text{ }\ket{v}\text{ }\bigg\}+A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\ket{u}\bigg)\text{ , }

implying that the form of the cost function is the following superposition,

\displaystyle\boxed{\mathcal{C}^{\mathrm{DSW}}_{v}\text{ }(\lambda,\lambda^{v}_{0})\text{ }}=\big(\lambda^{v}_{0}\big)^{2}+\text{ }\mathrm{Re}\text{ }\bigg\{\text{ }\big(\frac{2\tau}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\big)^{2}\text{ }\bra{\widetilde{v}}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{\widetilde{v}}\text{ }+\text{ }2\text{ }\big(\frac{2\tau}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\big)^{2}\text{ }\bra{\widetilde{v}}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{\widetilde{v}}\text{ }+\text{ }\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }\big(\frac{2\tau}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\big)^{2}\text{ }\bra{\widetilde{v}}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{\widetilde{v}}+\text{ }2\text{ }\big(\frac{2\tau}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\big)^{2}\text{ }\bra{\widetilde{v}}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{\widetilde{v}}\text{ }-\text{ }4\text{ }\big(\frac{2\tau}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\big)^{2}\text{ }\bra{\widetilde{v}}\ket{\widetilde{v}}\text{ }+\text{ }2\text{ }\big(\frac{2\tau}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\big)^{2}\text{ }\bra{\widetilde{v}}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{\widetilde{v}}\text{ }+\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle\text{ }4\text{ }\big(\frac{2\tau}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\big)^{2}\text{ }\bra{\widetilde{v}}A_{x}\ket{\widetilde{v}}\text{ }+\text{ }\big(\frac{2\tau}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\big)^{2}\text{ }\bra{\widetilde{v}}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{\widetilde{v}}\text{ }+\text{ }2\text{ }\big(\frac{2\tau}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\big)^{2}\text{ }\bra{v}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{\widetilde{v}}\text{ }-\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }4\text{ }\big(\frac{2\tau}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\big)^{2}\text{ }\bra{v}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{\widetilde{v}}\text{ }+\text{ }4\text{ }\big(\frac{2\tau}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\big)^{2}\text{ }\bra{v}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{\widetilde{v}}\text{ }+\text{ }2\text{ }\big(\frac{2\tau}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\big)^{2}\text{ }\bra{v}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{\widetilde{v}}\text{ }+\text{ }\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }4\text{ }\big(\frac{2\tau}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\big)^{2}\text{ }\bra{v}A_{x}\ket{\widetilde{v}}\text{ }+8\text{ }\big(\frac{2\tau}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\big)^{2}\text{ }\bra{v}\ket{\widetilde{v}}\text{ }-\text{ }4\text{ }\big(\frac{2\tau}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\big)^{2}\text{ }\bra{v}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{\widetilde{v}}-\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle 4\text{ }\big(\frac{2\tau}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\big)^{2}\text{ }\text{ }\bra{v}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{\widetilde{u}}\text{ }+\text{ }4\text{ }\big(\frac{2\tau}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\big)^{2}\text{ }\text{ }\bra{v}A_{x}\ket{\widetilde{u}}\text{ }-\text{ }2\text{ }\big(\frac{2\tau}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\big)^{2}\text{ }\bra{v}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{\widetilde{v}}\text{ }+\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }\big(\frac{2\tau}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\big)^{2}\text{ }\bra{v}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{v}\text{ }-\text{ }2\text{ }\big(\frac{2\tau}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\big)^{2}\text{ }\bra{v}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{v}\text{ }-\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle\text{ }4\text{ }\big(\frac{2\tau}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\big)^{2}\text{ }\bra{v}\ket{v}\text{ }-\text{ }4\text{ }\big(\frac{2\tau}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\big)^{2}\text{ }\bra{v}A_{x}\ket{v}\text{ }+\text{ }\frac{4\tau}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\text{ }A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{\widetilde{v}}\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }\bra{v}A_{x}\ket{v}\text{ }\bigg)-\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }\frac{8\text{ }\tau}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\text{ }\ket{\widetilde{v}}\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }\bra{v}A_{x}\ket{v}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }+\text{ }\frac{4\text{ }\tau}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\text{ }A_{x}\ket{\widetilde{v}}\bigg(\text{ }\bra{v}A_{x}\ket{v}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }-\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle\text{ }\frac{4\tau}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\text{ }\text{ }A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{v}\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }\bra{v}A_{x}\ket{v}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }+\text{ }\frac{4\tau}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\text{ }\ket{v}\bigg(\text{ }\bra{v}A_{x}\ket{v}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }-\text{ }\frac{2\tau}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\text{ }A_{x}\ket{v}\text{ }\bigg(\bra{v}A_{x}\ket{v}\bigg)\text{ }+\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle\text{ }A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }\bra{v}A_{x}\ket{v}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }-\text{ }\ket{u}\bigg(\text{ }\bra{v}A_{x}\ket{v}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }+\text{ }\lambda^{2}\text{ }\frac{4\tau}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\text{ }A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{\widetilde{v}}\text{ }-\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle\text{ }\lambda^{2}\text{ }\frac{8\text{ }\tau}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\text{ }\ket{\widetilde{v}}\text{ }+\text{ }\lambda^{2}\text{ }\frac{4\text{ }\tau}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\text{ }A_{x}\ket{\widetilde{v}}\text{ }+\text{ }\lambda^{2}\text{ }\frac{4\text{ }\tau}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\text{ }A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{v}\text{ }+\lambda^{2}\text{ }\frac{8\text{ }\tau}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\text{ }\ket{v}\text{ }+\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle\text{ }\lambda^{2}\text{ }\frac{4\text{ }\tau}{(\Delta x)^{3}}\text{ }A_{x}\ket{v}\text{ }A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{v}\text{ }+\text{ }\ket{v}\text{ }+\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }+\text{ }\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }2\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }+\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }4\ket{v}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }\bra{v}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{v}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }-\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }4\ket{v}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }\bra{v}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{v}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }\ket{u}\text{ }-\text{ }\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }\text{ }2\bra{v}\ket{v}\bigg(\text{ }A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{v}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }+\text{ }2\bra{v}\ket{v}\bigg(\text{ }\ket{v}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }\bigg\}\text{ }\text{ . }

for the second equation of the system. As a result, we obtain time evolved variational states for solutions
u,v
, which yield the following two cost functions, respectively denoted
\mathcal{C}_{u}
and
\mathcal{C}_{v}
. In comparison to other PDEs and the initial conditions under which they are solved in the library, we perform optimization for each cost function simultaneously to determine the ground state corresponding to solutions achieved classically.

Figure 71:
Extracting measurements from the DSW partial differential equation system for approximating traveling wave solutions
. Collecting extracted measurements corresponding to approximations of solutions to the DSW system with the Scipy Imfil optimizer yields approximations to one type of standing wave solution. For this particular solution, the traveling wave attains periodic maxima surrounding discontinuities of a few analytically known exact solutions from the tangent function. Solutions to each component of the PDE system are provided sequentially.

Figure 72:
Extracting measurements from the DSW partial differential equation system for approximating traveling wave solutions
. A continuation of solution approximations in continued from the previous figure, in which
v
solution approximations of the DSW system are approximated with a constrained optimization scheme from approximations of a
u
solution.

Figure 73:
Three-dimensional plots of solution curves from both components of the DSW system
. Components of solutions obtained in the previous two figures are presented in three-dimensional Euclidean space.

4.10
Hunter-Saxton equation

Cost function derivation

The partial differential equation reads,

\displaystyle\big(u_{t}+uu_{x}\big)_{x}=\frac{1}{2}u^{2}_{x}

\displaystyle\Updownarrow

\displaystyle\big(\ket{\widetilde{u}}+\ket{u}\ket{u}_{x}\big)_{x}=\bra{u}_{x}\frac{1}{2}\ket{u}_{x}

\displaystyle\Updownarrow

\displaystyle\big(\frac{A_{x}-\textbf{1}}{\Delta x}\big)\text{ }\big(\ket{\widetilde{u}}+\ket{u}\big(\text{ }\big(\text{ }\frac{A_{x}-\textbf{1}}{\Delta x}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\ket{u}\text{ }\text{ }\big)\text{ }=\big(\text{ }\frac{A_{x}-\textbf{1}}{\Delta x}\text{ }\big)\bra{u}\frac{1}{2}\big(\frac{A_{x}-\textbf{1}}{\Delta x}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\ket{u}

\displaystyle\Updownarrow

\displaystyle\big(\frac{A_{x}-\textbf{1}}{\Delta x}\big)\text{
}\ket{\widetilde{u}}+\big(\frac{A_{x}-\textbf{1}}{\Delta x}\big)\text{ }\ket{u}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\frac{A_{x}-\textbf{1}}{\Delta x}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\ket{u}\text{ }\text{ }=\frac{1}{2}\text{ }\big(\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\ket{u}}{\Delta x}\big)\text{ }\big(\frac{A_{x}\ket{u}-\ket{u}}{\Delta x}\big)

\displaystyle\Updownarrow

\displaystyle\frac{A_{x}\ket{u}-\ket{\widetilde{u}}}{\Delta x}+\big(\frac{A_{x}\ket{u}-\ket{u}}{\Delta x}\big)\text{ }\big(\frac{A_{x}\ket{u}-\ket{u}}{\Delta x}\big)\text{ }=\text{ }\frac{1}{2(\Delta x)^{2}}\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\bra{u}\ket{u}\text{ }\big)\text{ . }

We obtain the time evolved variational state by expanding one more superposition, followed by isolating for
\ket{\widetilde{u}}
,

\displaystyle\frac{A_{x}\ket{\widetilde{u}}-\ket{\widetilde{u}}}{\Delta x}+\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}-\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\bra{u}\ket{u}\text{ }\big)\text{ }=\frac{1}{2(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\bra{u}\ket{u}\text{ }\big)

\displaystyle\Updownarrow

\displaystyle\ket{\widetilde{u}}=-\text{ }\Delta x\bigg\{\frac{1}{2(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\bra{u}\ket{u}\text{ }\big)\text{ }-\text{ }\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}-\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\bra{u}\ket{u}\text{ }\big)-A_{x}\ket{u}_{t}\bigg\}\text{ . }

We compute the cost function below,

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}^{\text{ }\mathrm{H-S}\text{ }}(\lambda,\lambda_{0})=\bigg|\bigg|\ket{\widetilde{u}}-\ket{u}\bigg|\bigg|^{2}=\bigg|\bigg|-\text{ }\Delta x\bigg\{\frac{1}{2(\Delta x)^{2}}\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\bra{u}\ket{u}\text{ }\big)-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}-\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\bra{u}\ket{u}\text{ }\big)\text{ }-\text{ }A_{x}\ket{\widetilde{u}}\bigg\}-\ket{u}\bigg|\bigg|^{2}\text{ . }

Expanding the norm is equivalent to,

\displaystyle\bigg|\bigg|\text{ }-\frac{1}{2\Delta x}\text{ }\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\big(\frac{1}{2(\Delta x)}+\frac{1}{(\Delta x)}\big)\text{ }\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }-\cdots

\displaystyle\big(\frac{1}{2\Delta x}+\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)\bra{u}\ket{u}-\Delta x\text{ }A_{x}\ket{u}_{t}-\ket{u}\bigg|\bigg|^{2}

\displaystyle\Updownarrow

\displaystyle\text{ }\frac{1}{\Delta x}\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }\frac{1}{2}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\big(\frac{1}{2(\Delta x)}+\frac{1}{(\Delta x)}\big)\text{ }\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\text{ }\bigg)^{2}-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\Delta x}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle\big(\frac{1}{2(\Delta x)}+\frac{1}{(\Delta x)}\big)\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\bigg(\big(\frac{1}{2\Delta x}+\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)\bra{u}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle\Delta x\text{ }A_{x}\ket{\widetilde{u}}-\ket{u}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }+\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }\big(\text{ }\big(\frac{1}{2\Delta x}+\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)\text{ }\bra{u}\ket{u}-\text{ }\Delta x\text{ }A_{x}\ket{\widetilde{u}}-\ket{u}\text{ }\big)^{2}

We obtain,

\displaystyle\boxed{\mathcal{C}^{\text{ }\mathrm{H-S}\text{ }}(\lambda,\lambda^{\text{ }\mathrm{H-S}\text{ }}_{0})}=\text{ }\big(\lambda^{\text{ }\mathrm{H-S}\text{ }}_{0}\big)^{2}\text{ }+\mathrm{Re}\text{ }\bigg\{\text{ }\text{ }\frac{1}{4\Delta x}\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }-\text{ }\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }\frac{1}{2\Delta x}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\frac{1}{2\Delta x}+\frac{1}{\Delta x}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }-\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle\text{ }\frac{1}{2\Delta x}\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }\big(\text{ }\bigg(\frac{1}{2\Delta x}+\frac{1}{\Delta x}\text{ }\big)\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }+\text{ }\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }\frac{1}{\Delta x}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\frac{1}{2\Delta x}+\frac{1}{\Delta x}\text{ }\big)^{2}\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }\bra{v}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{v}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }\bra{v}A_{x}\ket{v}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }+\text{ }\frac{1}{2}\text{ }\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }\bra{u}\ket{u}\text{ }\times\cdots

\displaystyle\big(\frac{1}{2\Delta x}+\frac{1}{\Delta x}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\bigg)-\text{ }\text{ }\big(\text{ }\frac{1}{2\Delta x}+\frac{1}{\Delta x}\text{ }\big)\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\bigg(\text{ }A_{x}\ket{\widetilde{u}}\text{ }\bigg)-\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle\frac{\bra{u}}{\Delta x}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\frac{1}{2\Delta x}-\frac{1}{\Delta x}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\text{ }\frac{1}{\Delta x}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\frac{1}{2\Delta x}+\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }\bra{u}\ket{u}\text{ }\bigg)-\text{ }\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }\big(\text{ }\frac{1}{2\Delta x}+\frac{1}{\Delta x}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\bigg(\text{ }A_{x}\ket{\widetilde{u}}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }+\text{ }\frac{1}{\Delta x}\text{ }\big(\frac{1}{2\Delta x}+\frac{1}{\Delta x}\big)\text{ }\bra{u}\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\bigg)+\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle\text{ }\frac{1}{\Delta x}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\frac{1}{2\Delta x}+\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big)^{2}\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }\bra{u}\ket{u}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }\bra{u}\ket{u}\text{ }\bigg)-\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle\text{ }\text{ }\big(\text{ }\frac{1}{2\Delta x}+\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }\bra{u}\ket{u}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }A_{x}\ket{\widetilde{u}}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }+\text{ }\bra{u}\text{ }\frac{1}{\Delta x}\text{ }\big(\frac{1}{2\Delta x}+\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\bra{u}\ket{u}\text{ }+\text{ }\cdots

\displaystyle\bra{u}\ket{u}\text{ }\frac{1}{\Delta x}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\frac{1}{2\Delta x}+\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big)^{2}\text{ }\bra{u}\ket{u}+\big(\text{ }\frac{1}{2\Delta x}+\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big)\bra{u}\ket{u}\bigg(\text{ }A_{x}\ket{\widetilde{u}}\text{ }\bigg)-\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle-\text{ }\bra{u}\big(\text{ }\frac{1}{2\Delta x}+\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\bra{u}\ket{u}\text{ }-\text{ }\big(\text{ }\frac{1}{2\Delta x}+\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\bra{u}\ket{u}\text{ }+\cdots

\displaystyle(\Delta x)^{2}\text{ }\bra{\widetilde{u}}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }+\bra{u}\Delta xA_{x}\ket{\widetilde{u}}\text{ }-\cdots

\displaystyle\bra{u}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\frac{1}{2\Delta x}+\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big)\text{ }+2\text{ }\bra{u}\ket{u}+\bra{u}\Delta xA_{x}\ket{\widetilde{u}}\text{ }\bigg\}\text{ }\text{ . }

The desired cost function is given by the ultimate linear combination of expectation values above.
