4.11
KdV-Burgers equation

Cost function derivation

The equation reads,

\displaystyle\partial_{t}u+\mu\partial^{3}_{x}u+2u\partial_{x}u-\nu\partial^{2}_{x}u=0\text{ }\text{ , }

for real
\mu
and
\nu
, can be rearranged to obtain the cost function upon isolating for the time-evolved variational state of the solution from the following equation,

\displaystyle\ket{\widetilde{u}}=-\frac{\mu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }A_{x}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\ket{u}-2\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\frac{\nu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\ket{u}\text{ }\text{ , }

implying,

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}^{\mathrm{KdV-B}}(\lambda_{0},\lambda)=\big|\big|\ket{\widetilde{u}}-\ket{u}\big|\big|^{2}

\displaystyle=\bigg|\bigg|\big(-\frac{\mu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }A_{x}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\ket{u}-2\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\frac{\nu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\ket{u}\big)-\ket{u}\bigg|\bigg|^{2}

\displaystyle=\big(-\frac{\mu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }A_{x}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\ket{u}-2\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\frac{\nu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\ket{u}\big)^{2}+\bra{u}\ket{u}+\lambda_{0}^{2}

\displaystyle=\big(-\frac{\mu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }A_{x}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\ket{u}-2\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\big)^{2}+\cdots

\displaystyle\big(\frac{\nu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\ket{u}\big)^{2}+2\big(\frac{\nu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\ket{u}\big)\text{ }\big(-\frac{\mu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }A_{x}\times\cdots

\displaystyle\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\ket{u}-2\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\big)+\bra{u}\ket{u}+\lambda_{0}^{2}\text{ }\text{ , }

which, upon further rearrangement, is equivalent to,

\displaystyle\big(\big(-\frac{\mu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }A_{x}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\ket{u}\big)^{2}+\big(-2\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\big)^{2}+2\big(-\frac{\mu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }A_{x}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle 2\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\big)\text{ }\big(-2\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\big)\big)+\cdots

\displaystyle\big(\frac{\nu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}-2A^{\dagger}_{x}+A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}-2A^{\dagger}_{x}-4\textbf{1}-2A_{x}+A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}-2A_{x}+A_{x}A_{x}\big)\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{2\mu\nu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big(\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\ket{u}A_{x}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\ket{u}+\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\ket{u}\big(2\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\big)\big)+\cdots

\displaystyle\bra{u}\ket{u}+\lambda_{0}^{2}

\displaystyle=\big(\big(-\frac{\mu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\text{ }A_{x}A_{x}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}-2A^{\dagger}_{x}+A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}-2A^{\dagger}_{x}-4\textbf{1}-2A_{x}+A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}-2A_{x}+A_{x}A_{x}\big)\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle 4\big(\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\big)\big(\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\big)-\frac{2\mu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big(A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\big(-2\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\big)-2\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\big(-2\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\big)\big)\big)+\cdots

\displaystyle\big(\frac{\nu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2\big(\frac{\nu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\big(\frac{\nu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-2\big(\frac{\nu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-4\big(\frac{\nu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle 2\big(\frac{\nu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}A_{x}\ket{u}+\big(\frac{\nu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2\big(\frac{\nu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}A_{x}\ket{u}+\big(\frac{\nu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }\frac{2\mu\nu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big(\bra{u}\big(A_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)A^{\dagger}_{x}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\ket{u}+\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2\ket{u}+A_{x}\ket{u}\big)\big(2\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\big)\big)+\cdots

\displaystyle\bra{u}\ket{u}+\lambda_{0}^{2}

\displaystyle\approx\lambda_{0}^{2}+\big(-\frac{\mu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\text{ }A_{x}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2\big(-\frac{\mu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}A_{x}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\big(-\frac{\mu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}A_{x}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle 2\big(-\frac{\mu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}A_{x}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-4\big(-\frac{\mu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle 2\big(-\frac{\mu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}A_{x}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}+\big(-\frac{\mu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}A_{x}A_{x}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle 2\big(-\frac{\mu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}A_{x}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}+\big(-\frac{\mu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}A_{x}A_{x}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}+4\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{4\mu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\frac{8\mu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle\big(\frac{\nu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2\big(\frac{\nu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\big(\frac{\nu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-2\big(\frac{\nu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-4\big(\frac{\nu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle 2\big(\frac{\nu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}A_{x}\ket{u}+\big(\frac{\nu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2\big(\frac{\nu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}A_{x}\ket{u}+\big(\frac{\nu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{2\mu\nu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\big(A_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)A^{\dagger}_{x}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\ket{u}+\frac{2\mu\nu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2\ket{u}+A_{x}\ket{u}\big)\big(2\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\big)\text{ }\text{ , }

from which further rearrangements to the last expectation below yield,

\displaystyle\approx\big(-\frac{\mu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\text{ }A_{x}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2\big(-\frac{\mu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}A_{x}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\big(-\frac{\mu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}A_{x}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle 2\big(-\frac{\mu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}A_{x}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-4\big(-\frac{\mu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle 2\big(-\frac{\mu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}A_{x}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}+\big(-\frac{\mu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}A_{x}A_{x}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle 2\big(-\frac{\mu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}A_{x}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}+\big(-\frac{\mu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}A_{x}A_{x}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}+4\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{4\mu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\frac{8\mu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle\big(\frac{\nu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2\big(\frac{\nu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\big(\frac{\nu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-2\big(\frac{\nu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-4\big(\frac{\nu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle 2\big(\frac{\nu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}A_{x}\ket{u}+\big(\frac{\nu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2\big(\frac{\nu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}A_{x}\ket{u}+\big(\frac{\nu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{2\mu\nu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\underset{\big(A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}-2A^{\dagger}_{x}+A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)\text{ }\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)}{\underset{\underbrace{\big(A_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)A^{\dagger}_{x}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)}}{\underbrace{\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}-2A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}+A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}-2A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}-4A^{\dagger}_{x}-2A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}+A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}-2A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}+A_{x}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)}}}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{4\mu\nu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2\frac{4\mu\nu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}

\displaystyle+\frac{4\mu\nu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}+\lambda_{0}^{2}

\displaystyle\Updownarrow

\displaystyle\boxed{\mathcal{C}^{\mathrm{KdV-B}}(\lambda_{0},\lambda)}\approx\mathrm{Re}\big\{\big(-\frac{\mu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\text{ }A_{x}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2\big(-\frac{\mu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}A_{x}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\big(-\frac{\mu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}A_{x}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle 2\big(-\frac{\mu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}A_{x}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-4\big(-\frac{\mu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle 2\big(-\frac{\mu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}A_{x}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}+\big(-\frac{\mu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}A_{x}A_{x}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle 2\big(-\frac{\mu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}A_{x}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}+\big(-\frac{\mu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}A_{x}A_{x}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}+4\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{4\mu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\frac{8\mu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle\big(\frac{\nu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2\big(\frac{\nu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\big(\frac{\nu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-2\big(\frac{\nu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-4\big(\frac{\nu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle 2\big(\frac{\nu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}A_{x}\ket{u}+\big(\frac{\nu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2\big(\frac{\nu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}A_{x}\ket{u}+\big(\frac{\nu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{2\mu\nu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\frac{4\mu\nu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\frac{2\mu\nu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{4\mu\nu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\frac{8\mu\nu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\frac{4\mu\nu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\frac{2\mu\nu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{4\mu\nu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\frac{2\mu\nu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{4\mu\nu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2\frac{4\mu\nu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\frac{4\mu\nu}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}\big\}

\displaystyle+\lambda_{0}^{2}

4.12
non-homogeneous KdV equation

Cost function derivation

The equation reads,

\displaystyle\partial_{t}u+\alpha u+\beta\partial_{x}u+\gamma\partial^{2}_{x}u=\mathrm{Ai}(x)\text{ }\text{ , }

which can be rearranged to obtain the cost function upon isolating for the time-evolved variational state of the solution from the following equation,

\displaystyle\ket{\widetilde{u}}=-\alpha\ket{u}-\frac{\beta}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\ket{u}-\frac{\gamma}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\ket{u}+\mathrm{Ai}(x)\text{ }\text{ , }

implying,

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}^{\mathrm{n-hKdV}}(\lambda_{0},\lambda)=\big|\big|\ket{\widetilde{u}}-\ket{u}\big|\big|^{2}

\displaystyle=\bigg|\bigg|-\alpha\ket{u}-\frac{\beta}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\ket{u}-\frac{\gamma}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\ket{u}+\mathrm{Ai}(\lambda)-\ket{u}\bigg|\bigg|^{2}\text{ }

\displaystyle=\big(-\alpha\ket{u}-\frac{\beta}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\ket{u}\big)^{2}+\cdots

\displaystyle\big(-\frac{\gamma}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\ket{u}+\mathrm{Ai}(\lambda)-\ket{u}\big)^{2}+2\big(-\alpha\ket{u}-\frac{\beta}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\times\cdots

\displaystyle\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\ket{u}\big)\big(\frac{\gamma}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\ket{u}+\mathrm{Ai}(\lambda)-\ket{u}\big)

\displaystyle\approx\bra{u}\alpha^{2}\ket{u}+2\big(-\alpha\ket{u}\big)\text{ }\big(-\frac{\beta}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2\ket{u}+A_{x}\ket{u}\big)\big)+\cdots

\displaystyle\big(-\frac{\beta}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2\ket{u}+A_{x}\ket{u}\big)^{2}+\big(-\frac{\gamma}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\ket{u}\big)^{2}+\cdots

\displaystyle\big(\mathrm{Ai}(x)-\ket{u}\big)^{2}+2\big(-\frac{\gamma}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\ket{u}\big)\text{ }\big(\mathrm{Ai}(\lambda)-\ket{u}\big)+\cdots

\displaystyle 2\big(\alpha\ket{u}\big(\frac{\gamma}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\ket{u}\big)-\alpha\ket{u}\big(\mathrm{Ai}(\lambda)-\ket{u}\big)-\cdots

\displaystyle\big(\frac{\beta}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\ket{u}\big)\text{ }\big(\frac{\gamma}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\ket{u}\big)+\cdots

\displaystyle\big(\mathrm{Ai}(\lambda)\big)^{2}-2\mathrm{Ai}(\lambda)\ket{u}+\bra{u}\ket{u}\big)

\displaystyle\approx\bra{u}\alpha^{2}\ket{u}+\frac{2\beta\alpha}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}-4\alpha\bra{u}\ket{u}-2\alpha\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle\big(-\frac{\beta}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2\ket{u}+A_{x}\ket{u}\big)^{2}+\big(\big(-\frac{\gamma}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\ket{u}\big)^{2}+\cdots

\displaystyle\big(\mathrm{Ai}(\lambda)\big)^{2}-2\mathrm{Ai}(\lambda)\ket{u}+\bra{u}\ket{u}+2\big(-\frac{\gamma}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\ket{u}\big)\text{ }\big(\mathrm{Ai}(\lambda)-\ket{u}\big)+\cdots

\displaystyle 2\alpha\ket{u}\big(\frac{\gamma}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\ket{u}\big)-2\alpha\ket{u}\big(\mathrm{Ai}(\lambda)-\ket{u}\big)-\cdots

\displaystyle\big(\frac{2\beta}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)\text{ }\big(\frac{\gamma}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)\bra{u}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}-2A_{x}+A_{x}A_{x}-2A_{x}-4\textbf{1}-\cdots

\displaystyle 2A^{\dagger}_{x}+A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}-2A^{\dagger}_{x}+A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle\big(\mathrm{Ai}(\lambda)\big)^{2}-2\mathrm{Ai}(\lambda)\ket{u}+\bra{u}\ket{u}

Proceeding, approximately one has,

\displaystyle\bra{u}\alpha^{2}\ket{u}+\frac{2\beta\alpha}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}-4\alpha\bra{u}\ket{u}-2\alpha\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle\big(-\frac{\beta}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\big(\underset{\bra{u}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2\big(-2\ket{u}+A_{x}\ket{u}\big)\text{ }A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }+\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}}{\underbrace{\bra{u}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+4\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}+\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\text{ }+\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}}}\big)+\cdots

\displaystyle\big(-\frac{\beta}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\big(\underset{\bra{u}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2\big(-2\ket{u}+A_{x}\ket{u}\big)\text{ }A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }+\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}}{\underbrace{\bra{u}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+4\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}+\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}+\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}}}\big)+\big(-\frac{\gamma}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2\times\cdots

\displaystyle\big(-\frac{\gamma}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle\big(-\frac{\gamma}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-2\big(-\frac{\gamma}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-4\big(-\frac{\gamma}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}-2\big(-\frac{\gamma}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}A_{x}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle\big(-\frac{\gamma}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2\big(-\frac{\gamma}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}A_{x}\ket{u}-2\big(-\frac{\gamma}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle\big(\mathrm{Ai}(\lambda)\big)^{2}-2\mathrm{Ai}(\lambda)\ket{u}+\bra{u}\ket{u}+2\big(-\frac{\gamma}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\ket{u}\big)\text{ }\big(\mathrm{Ai}(\lambda)-\ket{u}\big)+\cdots

\displaystyle 2\alpha\ket{u}\big(\frac{\gamma}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\ket{u}\big)-2\alpha\ket{u}\big(\mathrm{Ai}(\lambda)-\ket{u}\big)-\cdots

\displaystyle\big(\frac{2\beta}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)\text{ }\big(\frac{\gamma}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)\bra{u}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}-2A_{x}+A_{x}A_{x}-2A_{x}-4\textbf{1}-\cdots

\displaystyle 2A^{\dagger}_{x}+A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}-2A^{\dagger}_{x}+A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle\big(\mathrm{Ai}(\lambda)\big)^{2}-2\mathrm{Ai}(\lambda)\ket{u}+\bra{u}\ket{u}

\displaystyle\Updownarrow

\displaystyle\boxed{\mathcal{C}^{\mathrm{n-hKdV}}(\lambda_{0},\lambda)}\approx\mathrm{Re}\big\{\bra{u}\alpha^{2}\ket{u}+\frac{2\beta\alpha}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}-4\alpha\bra{u}\ket{u}-2\alpha\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle\big(-\frac{\beta}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\bra{u}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+4\big(-\frac{\beta}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle\big(-\frac{\beta}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\text{ }+\big(-\frac{\beta}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle\big(-\frac{\beta}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\bra{u}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+4\big(-\frac{\beta}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle\big(-\frac{\beta}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle\big(-\frac{\beta}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}+\big(-\frac{\gamma}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2\big(-\frac{\gamma}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle\big(-\frac{\gamma}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-2\big(-\frac{\gamma}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-4\big(-\frac{\gamma}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}-2\big(-\frac{\gamma}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}A_{x}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle\big(-\frac{\gamma}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2\big(-\frac{\gamma}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}A_{x}\ket{u}-2\big(-\frac{\gamma}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle\big(\mathrm{Ai}(\lambda)\big)^{2}-2\mathrm{Ai}(\lambda)\ket{u}+\bra{u}\ket{u}-2\big(\frac{\gamma}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)\mathrm{Ai}(\lambda)A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle 4\mathrm{Ai}(\lambda)\ket{u}-2\big(\frac{\gamma}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)\mathrm{Ai}(\lambda)A_{x}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle 2\big(\frac{\gamma}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2\big(\frac{\gamma}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)\mathrm{Ai}(\lambda)\ket{u}-2\big(\frac{\gamma}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)\mathrm{Ai}(\lambda)\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}

\displaystyle 2\alpha\frac{\gamma}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}-4\alpha\frac{\gamma}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\ket{u}+2\alpha\frac{\gamma}{(\Delta x)^{2}}+2\alpha\frac{\gamma}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle 2\alpha\mathrm{Ai}(\lambda)\ket{u}+2\alpha\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle\big(\frac{2\beta}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)\text{ }\big(\frac{\gamma}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)\bra{u}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2\big(\frac{2\beta}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)\text{ }\big(\frac{\gamma}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle\big(\frac{2\beta}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)\text{ }\big(\frac{\gamma}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-2\big(\frac{2\beta}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)\text{ }\big(\frac{\gamma}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle 4\big(\frac{2\beta}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)\text{ }\big(\frac{\gamma}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)\bra{u}\ket{u}-2\big(\frac{2\beta}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)\text{ }\big(\frac{\gamma}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle\big(\frac{2\beta}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)\text{ }\big(\frac{\gamma}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-2\big(\frac{2\beta}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)\text{ }\big(\frac{\gamma}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\big(\frac{2\beta}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)\text{ }\big(\frac{\gamma}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle\big(\mathrm{Ai}(\lambda)\big)^{2}-2\mathrm{Ai}(\lambda)\ket{u}+\bra{u}\ket{u}\big\}+\lambda_{0}^{2}\text{ }\text{ . }

4.13
A generalized KdV equation

Cost function derivation

The equation reads,

\displaystyle\partial_{t}u+\partial^{3}_{x}u=\partial^{5}_{x}u\text{ }\text{ , }

which can be rearranged to obtain the cost function upon isolating for the time-evolved variational state of the solution from the following equation,

\displaystyle\ket{\widetilde{u}}=\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}A_{x}A_{x}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\ket{u}-\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}A_{x}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\ket{u}

\displaystyle\Updownarrow

\displaystyle\ket{\widetilde{u}}=\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big(A_{x}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}-2A_{x}A_{x}+A_{x}A_{x}A_{x}\big)\ket{u}-\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big(A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}-2A_{x}+A_{x}A_{x}\big)\ket{u}\text{ }\text{ , }

implying,

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}^{\mathrm{gKdV}}(\lambda_{0},\lambda)=\big|\big|\ket{\widetilde{u}}-\ket{u}\big|\big|^{2}

\displaystyle=\bigg|\bigg|\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big(A_{x}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}-2A_{x}A_{x}+A_{x}A_{x}A_{x}\big)\ket{u}-\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big(A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}-2A_{x}+A_{x}A_{x}\big)\ket{u}-\ket{u}\bigg|\bigg|^{2}

\displaystyle=\big(\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big(A_{x}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}-2A_{x}A_{x}+A_{x}A_{x}A_{x}\big)\ket{u}-\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big(A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}-2A_{x}+A_{x}A_{x}\big)\ket{u}\big)^{2}+\bra{u}\ket{u}+\lambda_{0}^{2}

\displaystyle=\underset{(*)}{\underbrace{\big(\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big(A_{x}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}-2A_{x}A_{x}+A_{x}A_{x}A_{x}\big)\ket{u}\big)^{2}}}+\underset{(**)}{\underbrace{\big(-\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big(A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}-2A_{x}+A_{x}A_{x}\big)\ket{u}\big)^{2}}}+\cdots

\displaystyle 2\underset{(***)}{\underbrace{\big(\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big(A_{x}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}-2A_{x}A_{x}+A_{x}A_{x}A_{x}\big)\ket{u}\big)\text{ }\big(\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big(A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}-2A_{x}+A_{x}A_{x}\big)\ket{u}\big)}}+\bra{u}\ket{u}+\lambda_{0}^{2}

\displaystyle\boxed{\mathcal{C}^{\mathrm{gKdV}}(\lambda_{0},\lambda)}\approx\lambda_{0}^{2}+\mathrm{Re}\big\{\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}A^{3}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}A^{3}_{x}\ket{u}+\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}A^{4}_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}A^{2}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\frac{4}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}A^{2}_{x}\ket{u}-\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}A^{3}_{x}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{3}A^{2}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{3}A^{2}_{x}\ket{u}+\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x})^{3}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}+\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\big(A_{x}\big)^{3}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\frac{4}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}A_{x}\ket{u}+\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\big(A_{x}\big)^{3}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\big(A_{x}\big)^{3}\ket{u}+\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\big(A_{x}\big)^{4}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\frac{4}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}+\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\big(A_{x}\big)^{3}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}+\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\big(A_{x}\big)^{3}\ket{u}\big\}\text{ }\text{ , }

where, before obtaining the final superposition for
\mathcal{C}^{\mathrm{gKdV}}
, for
(*)
,

\displaystyle(*)\equiv\bra{u}\big(A_{x}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}-2A_{x}A_{x}+A_{x}A_{x}A_{x}\big)^{\dagger}\text{ }\big(A_{x}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}-2A_{x}A_{x}+A_{x}A_{x}A_{x}\big)\ket{u}

\displaystyle=\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}-2A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}+A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)\text{ }\big(A_{x}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}-2A_{x}A_{x}+A_{x}A_{x}A_{x}\big)\ket{u}

\displaystyle=\bra{u}\bigg(\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\big)\big(A_{x}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)+\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\big)\big(-2A_{x}A_{x}\big)+\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\big)\big(A_{x}A_{x}A_{x}\big)-\cdots

\displaystyle\big(2A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)\big(A_{x}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)-\big(2A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)\big(-2A_{x}A_{x}\big)-\big(2A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)\big(A_{x}A_{x}A_{x}\big)+\cdots

\displaystyle\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)\big(A_{x}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)+\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)\big(-2A_{x}A_{x}\big)+\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)\big(A_{x}A_{x}A_{x}\big)\bigg)\ket{u}\text{ }\text{ , }

while for
(**)
,

\displaystyle(**)\equiv\big(\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\big)\big(\bra{u}\big(A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}-2A_{x}+A_{x}A_{x}\big)^{\dagger}\big(A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}-2A_{x}+A_{x}A_{x}\big)\ket{u}\big)

\displaystyle=\big(\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\big)\big(\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}-2A^{\dagger}_{x}+A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)\text{ }\big(A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}-2A_{x}+A_{x}A_{x}\big)\ket{u}

\displaystyle=\big(\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\big)\bra{u}\bigg(\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\big)\big(A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)+\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\big)\big(-2A_{x}\big)+\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\big)\big(A_{x}A_{x}\big)-\cdots

\displaystyle\big(2A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)\big(A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)-\big(2A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)\big(-2A_{x}\big)-\big(2A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)\big(A_{x}A_{x}\big)+\cdots

\displaystyle\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)\big(A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)+\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)\big(-2A_{x}\big)+\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)\big(A_{x}A_{x}\big)\bigg)\ket{u}\text{ }\text{ , }

and finally, for
(***)
,

\displaystyle(***)\equiv\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\big(A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}-2A_{x}+A_{x}A_{x}\big)^{\dagger}\text{ }\big(A_{x}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}-2A_{x}A_{x}+A_{x}A_{x}A_{x}\big)\ket{u}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}-2A^{\dagger}_{x}+A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)\big(A_{x}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}-2A_{x}A_{x}+A_{x}A_{x}A_{x}\big)\ket{u}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\bigg(\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\big)\big(A_{x}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)+\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\big)\big(-2A_{x}A_{x}\big)+\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\big)\big(A_{x}A_{x}A_{x}\big)-\cdots

\displaystyle\big(2A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)\big(A_{x}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)-\big(2A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)\big(-2A_{x}A_{x}\big)+\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)\big(A_{x}A_{x}A_{x}\big)+\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)\big(A_{x}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)+\cdots

\displaystyle\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)\big(-2A_{x}A_{x}\big)+\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)\big(A_{x}A_{x}A_{x}\big)\bigg)\ket{u}\text{ }\text{ . }

4.14
KdV equation

Cost function derivation

The equation reads,

\displaystyle\partial_{t}u+\partial^{3}_{x}u+6u\partial_{x}u=0\text{ }\text{ , }

which can be rearranged to obtain the cost function upon isolating for the time-evolved variational state of the solution from the following equation,

\displaystyle\ket{\widetilde{u}}=-\frac{6}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\big(A_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)\ket{u}-\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}A_{x}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\ket{u}

\displaystyle=-\frac{6}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\big(A_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)\ket{u}-\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big(A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}-2A_{x}+\cdots

\displaystyle A_{x}A_{x}\big)\ket{u}\text{ }\text{ , }

implying,

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}^{\mathrm{KdV}}(\lambda_{0},\lambda)=\big|\big|\ket{\widetilde{u}}-\ket{u}\big|\big|^{2}

\displaystyle=\bigg|\bigg|\big(-\frac{6}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\big(A_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)\ket{u}-\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big(A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}-2A_{x}+\cdots

\displaystyle A_{x}A_{x}\big)\ket{u}\big)-\ket{u}\bigg|\bigg|^{2}

\displaystyle=\bigg|\bigg|-\frac{6}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}+\frac{12}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\ket{u}-\frac{6}{(\Delta x)^{2}}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{2}}A_{x}\ket{u}-\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-\ket{u}\bigg|\bigg|^{2}

\displaystyle=\big(-\frac{6}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}+\frac{12}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\ket{u}-\frac{6}{(\Delta x)^{2}}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\big)^{2}+2\big(-\frac{6}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}+\frac{12}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\ket{u}-\frac{6}{(\Delta x)^{2}}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\big)\times\cdots

\displaystyle\big(\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{2}}A_{x}\ket{u}-\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-\ket{u}\big)+\big(\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{2}}A_{x}\ket{u}-\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-\ket{u}\big)^{2}+\lambda_{0}^{2}\text{ }\text{ , }

which, upon further rearrangement, is approximately equivalent to the superposition,

\displaystyle\big(-\frac{6}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}+\frac{12}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\ket{u}-\frac{6}{(\Delta x)^{2}}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\big)^{2}+2\big(-\frac{6}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}+\frac{12}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\ket{u}-\frac{6}{(\Delta x)^{2}}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\big)\times\cdots

\displaystyle\big(\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{2}}A_{x}\ket{u}-\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-\ket{u}\big)+\big(\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{2}}A_{x}\ket{u}-\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-\ket{u}\big)^{2}

\displaystyle\approx\big(-\frac{6}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}+\frac{12}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\ket{u}\big)^{2}+2\big(-\frac{6}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}+\frac{12}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\ket{u}\big)\times\cdots

\displaystyle\big(-\frac{6}{(\Delta x)^{2}}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\big)+\big(-\frac{6}{(\Delta x)^{2}}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\big)^{2}+\cdots

\displaystyle 2\big(-\frac{6}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}+\frac{12}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}-\frac{6}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\big)+\cdots

\displaystyle 2\big(-\frac{6}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}+\frac{12}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{6}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\big)-\cdots

\displaystyle 2\big(-\frac{6}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}+\frac{12}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\ket{u}-\frac{6}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\big)+\big(\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\big(2\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}+\big(A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-\ket{u}\big)\text{ }\big(2A_{x}\ket{u}\big)+\cdots

\displaystyle\big(A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-\ket{u}\big)^{2}\big)\text{ }+\lambda_{0}^{2}\text{ , }

which, upon expanding, and collecting, all remaining like-terms, is, approximately,

\displaystyle\big(-\frac{6}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}-\frac{72}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}+\frac{144}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}\ket{u}\big)+\big(-\frac{12}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}+\frac{24}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{72}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\frac{24}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\big)+\big(\big(-\frac{6}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\bra{u}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\frac{12}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\big)+\cdots

\displaystyle 2\big(-\frac{6}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}+\frac{12}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}-\frac{6}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\big)+\cdots

\displaystyle 2\big(-\frac{6}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}+\frac{12}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{6}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\big)-\cdots

\displaystyle 2\big(-\frac{6}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}+\frac{12}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\ket{u}-\frac{6}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\big)+\cdots

\displaystyle\big(\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\big(2\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}+\big(\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\big)\text{ }+\cdots

\displaystyle\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-\bra{u}\ket{u}-2\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}\big)+\lambda_{0}^{2}\text{ }\text{ , }

finally yielding,

\displaystyle\boxed{\mathcal{C}^{\mathrm{KdV}}(\lambda_{0},\lambda)}\approx\lambda_{0}^{2}-\mathrm{Re}\big\{\frac{6}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}-\frac{72}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}+\frac{144}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{12}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}+\frac{24}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}-\frac{72}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{24}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\frac{36}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{12}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{12}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}+\frac{24}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{12}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }-\frac{4}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{12}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}+\frac{24}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{12}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{12}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}+\frac{24}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\ket{u}-\frac{12}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }+\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle 2\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}\big\}\text{ }\text{ . }

4.15
Translational KdV equation

Cost function derivation

The equation reads,

\displaystyle\partial_{t}u+\partial^{3}_{x}u-6f(t)u\partial_{x}u=0\text{ }\text{ , }

which, for
f(t)\equiv\frac{1}{6}
, yields an identical cost function to that which was previously provided for the KdV equation,

\displaystyle\boxed{\mathcal{C}^{\mathrm{translational}}(\lambda_{0},\lambda)}\approx\lambda_{0}^{2}-\mathrm{Re}\big\{\frac{6}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}-\frac{72}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}+\frac{144}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{12}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}+\frac{24}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}-\frac{72}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{24}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\frac{36}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{12}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{12}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}+\frac{24}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{12}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }-\frac{4}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{12}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}+\frac{24}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{12}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{12}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}+\frac{24}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\ket{u}-\frac{12}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+2\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }+\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle 2\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}\big\}\text{. }

4.16
super KdV equation

Cost functions derivation

The system of equations reads, for solutions
(u,w)
,

\displaystyle\partial_{t}u=6u\partial_{x}u-\partial^{3}_{x}u+3w\partial^{2}_{x}w\text{ }\text{ , }

\displaystyle\partial_{t}w=3(\partial_{x}u)w+6u\partial_{x}w-4\partial^{3}_{x}w\text{ }\text{ , }

which, upon the following transformation,

\displaystyle\ket{\widetilde{u}}=6\big(\Delta x\big)^{-2}\ket{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\ket{u}-\big(\Delta x\big)^{-2}A_{x}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\ket{u}+3\big(\Delta x\big)^{-2}\ket{w}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\ket{w}\text{ , }\text{ }

\displaystyle\equiv 6\big(\Delta x\big)^{-2}\bra{u}\big(A_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)\ket{u}-\big(\Delta x\big)^{-2}\big(A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}-2A_{x}+A_{x}A_{x}\big)\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle 3\big(\Delta x\big)^{-2}\bra{w}\big(A_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)\ket{w}\text{ }\text{ , }

corresponding to the first equation of the system, and,

\displaystyle\ket{\widetilde{w}}=3\big(\Delta x\big)^{-2}\bra{w}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\ket{u}+6\bra{w}\big(A_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)\ket{u}-4\big(\Delta x\big)^{-2}A_{x}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\ket{w}

\displaystyle=3\big(\Delta x\big)^{-2}\bra{w}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\ket{u}+6\bra{w}\big(A_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle 4\big(\Delta x\big)^{-2}\big(A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}-2A_{x}+A_{x}A_{x}\big)\ket{w}\text{ }\text{ , }

corresponding to the second equation of the system. Proceeding,

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}^{\mathrm{S}}_{u}(\lambda_{0},\lambda)=\big|\big|\ket{\widetilde{u}}-\ket{u}\big|\big|^{2}

\displaystyle=\bigg|\bigg|6\big(\Delta x\big)^{-2}\bra{u}\big(A_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)\ket{u}-\big(\Delta x\big)^{-2}\big(A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}-2A_{x}+A_{x}A_{x}\big)\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle 3\big(\Delta x\big)^{-2}\bra{w}\big(A_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)\ket{w}-\ket{u}\bigg|\bigg|^{2}

\displaystyle=\lambda_{0}^{2}+36\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\big(\bra{u}\big(A_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)\ket{u}\big)\text{ }\big(\bra{u}\big(A_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)\ket{u}\big)+\cdots

\displaystyle\big(-\big(\Delta x\big)^{-2}\big(A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}-2A_{x}+A_{x}A_{x}\big)\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle 3\big(-\big(\Delta x\big)^{-2}\bra{w}\big(A_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)\ket{w}-\ket{u}\big)+\cdots

\displaystyle\big(\big(\Delta x\big)^{-2}\big(A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}-2A_{x}+A_{x}A_{x}\big)\ket{u}+3\big(\Delta x\big)^{-2}\bra{w}\big(A_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)\ket{w}\big)^{2}

\displaystyle\approx\lambda^{2}_{0}+36\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\underset{(*)}{\underbrace{\big(\bra{u}\big(\text{ }\big(A_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)\text{ }\big(A_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)\ket{u}\big)}}-\cdots

\displaystyle\big(\Delta x\big)^{-2}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+2\big(-\big(\Delta x\big)^{-2}A_{x}\ket{u}-\big(\Delta x\big)^{-2}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle 3\big(\Delta x\big)^{-2}\bra{w}A_{x}\ket{w}+6\big(\Delta x\big)^{-2}\bra{w}\ket{w}-3\big(\Delta x\big)^{-2}\bra{w}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{w}-\cdots

\displaystyle\ket{u}\big)+\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\underset{(**)}{\underbrace{\bra{u}\big(A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}-2A_{x}+A_{x}A_{x}\big)^{\dagger}\big(A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}-2A_{x}+A_{x}A_{x}\big)\ket{u}}}+\cdots

\displaystyle 9\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\underset{(***)}{\underbrace{\bra{w}\big(A_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)\big(A_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)\ket{w}}}+\cdots

\displaystyle 6\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\underset{(****)}{\underbrace{\big(\big(A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}-2A_{x}+A_{x}A_{x}\big)\ket{u}\big)\text{ }\big(\bra{w}\big(A_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)\ket{w}\big)}}\big)

where, for
(*)
,

\displaystyle(*)\equiv\bra{u}\bigg(\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}-2A_{x}+A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}-2A_{x}+4\textbf{1}-2A^{\dagger}_{x}+A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}-2A^{\dagger}_{x}\bigg)\ket{u}

\displaystyle=\bra{u}\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}-2\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}+\bra{u}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}+4\bra{u}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle 2\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-2\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\text{ , }

while, for
(**)
,

\displaystyle(**)\equiv\bra{u}\bigg(\big(A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{\dagger}\big(A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)\big(-2A_{x}\big)+\big(A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{\dagger}\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}-\big(2A_{x}\big)^{\dagger}\big(A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)-\big(2A_{x}\big)^{\dagger}\big(2A_{x}\big)-\cdots

\displaystyle\big(2A_{x}\big)^{\dagger}\big(\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}\big)^{\dagger}+\big(\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}\big)^{\dagger}\big(A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)+\big(\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}\big)^{\dagger}\big(-2A_{x}\big)+\big(\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}\big)^{\dagger}\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}\bigg)\ket{u}

\displaystyle\approx-2\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}+\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\big(A_{x}\big)^{3}\ket{u}-2\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle 4\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-2\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{3}\ket{u}+\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle 2\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}A_{x}\ket{u}+\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}\text{ }\text{ , }

while, for
(***)
,

\displaystyle(***)\equiv\bra{w}\bigg(\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}-2A_{x}+A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}-2A_{x}-4\textbf{1}-2A^{\dagger}_{x}+A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}-2A^{\dagger}_{x}+\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\bigg)\ket{w}

\displaystyle=\bra{w}\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{w}-2\bra{w}A_{x}\ket{w}+\bra{w}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{w}-\cdots

\displaystyle 2\bra{w}A_{x}\ket{w}-4\bra{w}\ket{w}-2\bra{w}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{w}+\bra{w}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{w}-\cdots

\displaystyle 2\bra{w}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{w}+\bra{w}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{w}\text{ }\text{ , }

and finally, for
(****)
,

\displaystyle(****)\approx\bra{w}\big(A_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{\dagger}\text{ }\big(A_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)\ket{u}

\displaystyle\equiv\bra{w}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\text{ }\big(A_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)\ket{u}

\displaystyle\approx\bra{w}\bigg(A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}-2A^{\dagger}_{x}+\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}-2A_{x}+4\textbf{1}-2A^{\dagger}_{x}+\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}-2A_{x}+A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\bigg)\ket{u}

\displaystyle=\bra{w}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-2\bra{w}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\bra{w}\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}-2\bra{w}A_{x}\ket{u}-4\bra{w}\ket{u}-2\bra{w}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\bra{w}\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle 2\bra{w}A_{x}\ket{u}+\bra{w}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\text{ , }

implying, for the first equation of the system, that,

\displaystyle\boxed{\mathcal{C}^{\mathrm{S}}_{u}(\lambda_{0},\lambda)}\approx\lambda^{2}_{0}+\cdots

\displaystyle\mathrm{Re}\big\{36\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\bra{u}\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}-72\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}+36\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\bra{u}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-72\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle 144\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\bra{u}\ket{u}-72\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-72\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}

\displaystyle 2\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}+\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\big(A_{x}\big)^{3}\ket{u}-2\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle 2\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}A_{x}\ket{u}+\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle 24\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\bra{w}\ket{u}-12\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\bra{w}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+6\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\bra{w}\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle 12\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\bra{w}A_{x}\ket{u}+6\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\bra{w}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\big\}\text{ }\text{ , }

Corresponding to the second equation of the system,

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}^{\mathrm{S}}_{w}(\lambda_{0},\lambda)=\big|\big|\ket{\widetilde{w}}-\ket{w}\big|\big|^{2}

\displaystyle=\bigg|\bigg|3\big(\Delta x\big)^{-2}\bra{w}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\ket{u}+6\bra{w}\big(A_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }\bigg(4\big(\Delta x\big)^{-2}\big(A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}-2A_{x}+A_{x}A_{x}\big)\ket{w}-\ket{w}\bigg)+\lambda^{2}_{0}

\displaystyle\approx 9\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\big(\bra{w}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\text{ }\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)^{\dagger}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle 6\bra{w}\big(A_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)\text{ }\big(A_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{\dagger}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle 12\bra{w}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\text{ }\big(A_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{\dagger}\ket{u}\big)-\cdots

\displaystyle 16\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\ket{w}-2\bigg(12\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\bra{w}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\text{ }\times\cdots

\displaystyle 6\bra{w}\big(A_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)\ket{u}\bigg)+\lambda_{0}^{2}\text{ }\text{ , }

which, because of the fact that distributing terms to the last group of terms appearing before
\lambda^{2}_{0}

\displaystyle-24\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\bra{w}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\text{ }\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}-2A^{\dagger}_{x}+\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\big)\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle 96\big(\Delta x\big)^{-2}\bra{w}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-48\big(\Delta x\big)^{-2}\bra{w}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle 24\big(\Delta x\big)^{-2}\bra{w}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}A_{x}\ket{u}-48\big(\Delta x\big)^{-2}\bra{w}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle 24\big(\Delta x\big)^{-2}\bra{w}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{3}\ket{u}\text{ }\text{ , }

in addition to the fact that grouping remaining terms from the above superposition, which have prefactor
9\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}
, yields,

\displaystyle 9\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\text{ }\bra{w}\bigg(\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)\big(A_{x}\big)-2A^{\dagger}_{x}+\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}-2A^{\dagger}_{x}+4\textbf{1}-2A^{\dagger}_{x}+\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}-2A_{x}+\big(A_{x}\big)\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)\bigg)\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle 54\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\text{ }\bra{w}\bigg(A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}-2A_{x}+\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}-2A_{x}+4\textbf{1}-2A_{x}+A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\bigg)\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle 108\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\text{ }\bra{w}\bigg(\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}-2A^{\dagger}_{x}+\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}-2A^{\dagger}_{x}+4\textbf{1}-2A_{x}+A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}-2A_{x}+\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}\bigg)\ket{u}

\displaystyle\approx 9\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\bra{w}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-18\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\bra{w}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-18\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\bra{w}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle 36\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\bra{w}\ket{u}-18\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\bra{w}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+9\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\bra{w}\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle 18\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\bra{w}A_{x}\ket{u}+9\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\bra{w}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle 54\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\bra{w}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-108\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\bra{w}A_{x}\ket{u}+54\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\bra{w}\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle 108\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\bra{w}A_{x}\ket{u}+216\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\bra{w}\ket{u}-108\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\bra{w}A_{x}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle 54\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\bra{w}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}+108\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\bra{w}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}-532\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\bra{w}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle 108\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\bra{w}\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}+432\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\bra{w}\ket{u}-216\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\bra{w}A_{x}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle 108\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\bra{w}\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}\text{ }\text{ . }

As a result, the following approximate cost function takes the form,

\displaystyle\boxed{\mathcal{C}^{\mathrm{S}}_{w}(\lambda_{0},\lambda)}\approx\lambda^{2}_{0}-\mathrm{Re}\big\{24\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\bra{w}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}A_{x}\ket{u}-2\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\bra{w}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}+\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\bra{w}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{3}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle 2\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\bra{w}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-4\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\bra{w}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\bra{w}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}+\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\bra{w}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle 2\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\bra{w}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\bra{w}A_{x}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}+3\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\big(\Delta x\big)^{-2}\bra{w}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle 6\big(\Delta x\big)^{-2}\bra{w}\ket{u}+3\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\big(\Delta x\big)^{-2}\bra{w}A_{x}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle 24\big(\Delta x\big)^{-2}\bra{w}A^{\dagger}_{x}\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}-48\big(\Delta x\big)^{-2}\bra{w}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle 24\big(\Delta x\big)^{-2}\bra{w}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-48\big(\Delta x\big)^{-2}\bra{w}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle 96\big(\Delta x\big)^{-2}\bra{w}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-48\big(\Delta x\big)^{-2}\bra{w}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle 24\big(\Delta x\big)^{-2}\bra{w}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}A_{x}\ket{u}-48\big(\Delta x\big)^{-2}\bra{w}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle 24\big(\Delta x\big)^{-2}\bra{w}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{3}\ket{u}+9\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\bra{w}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle 18\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\bra{w}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-18\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\bra{w}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle 36\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\bra{w}\ket{u}-18\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\bra{w}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+9\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\bra{w}\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle 18\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\bra{w}A_{x}\ket{u}+9\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\bra{w}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle 54\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\bra{w}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-108\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\bra{w}A_{x}\ket{u}+54\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\bra{w}\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle 108\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\bra{w}A_{x}\ket{u}+216\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\bra{w}\ket{u}-108\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\bra{w}A_{x}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle 54\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\bra{w}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}+108\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\bra{w}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}-532\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\bra{w}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle 108\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\bra{w}\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}+432\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\bra{w}\ket{u}-216\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\bra{w}A_{x}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle 108\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\bra{w}\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}-16\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{w}-32\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}A_{x}\ket{w}+16\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{w}-\cdots

\displaystyle 16\big(\Delta x\big)^{-4}\ket{w}\big\}\text{ }\text{ . }
