4.17
Benney-Luke equation

Cost function derivation

For approximations of water wave evolution, the PDE is of the form,

\displaystyle u_{tt}=u_{xx}-\alpha u_{xxxx}+\beta u_{xxtt}-u_{t}u_{xx}-2u_{x}u_{xt}\text{ , }

where
\alpha,\beta>0
, and satisfy,

\displaystyle\alpha-\beta=\sigma-\frac{1}{3}\text{ , }

where
\sigma>0
is the surface tension. One readily obtains the time evolved variational state for the solution by isolating the second time derivative of the solution, in which,

\displaystyle\text{ }\big(\frac{\ket{u(t+\tau)}-\ket{u}}{\tau}\big)_{t}=\big(\text{ }\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\ket{u}-\alpha\text{ }\big(\text{ }\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big)_{xx}\text{ }\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle\beta\text{ }\big(\text{ }\big(\text{ }\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big)\ket{u}\text{ }\big)_{tt}\text{ }-\big(\text{ }\frac{\ket{u(t+\tau)}-\ket{u}}{\tau}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\big(\text{ }\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{\Delta x}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }2\text{ }\big(\text{ }\frac{A_{x}-\textbf{1}}{\Delta x}\text{ }\big)\ket{u}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\big(\text{ }\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-\textbf{1}}{\Delta x}\big)\text{ }\ket{u}\text{ }\big)_{t}

\displaystyle\text{ }\Updownarrow

\displaystyle\frac{\ket{u(t+2\tau)}-\ket{u}}{\tau^{2}}=\big(\text{ }\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big)\ket{u}-\text{ }\frac{\alpha}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\text{ }\big(\text{ }A^{\dagger}_{x}\text{ }\bigg\{A^{\dagger}_{x}\big(\text{ }A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\text{ }\big)\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }\text{ }\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\ket{u}\text{ }\bigg\}\text{ }-\bigg\{\text{ }A^{\dagger}_{x}\big(\text{ }A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle\big(\text{ }A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\ket{u}\text{ }\bigg\}\text{ }\big)\text{ }+\frac{\beta}{\tau^{2}}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\big(\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)\text{ }\ket{u(t+2\tau)}-\big(\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)\text{ }\ket{u}\text{ }\big)\text{ }-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\tau\Delta x}\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u(t+\tau)}-\bra{u}2\ket{u(t+\tau)}+\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}+2\bra{u}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\big)-\frac{2}{\tau}\big(\text{ }\frac{A_{x}-\textbf{1}}{\Delta x}\big)\text{ }\ket{u}\big(\text{ }\big(\text{ }A^{\dagger}_{x}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-\textbf{1}}{\Delta x}\big)\ket{u}\text{ }\big)\text{ }-\big(\text{ }\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-\textbf{1}}{\Delta x}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\ket{u}\text{ }\big)\text{ . }

Rearranging cross terms resulting from the time derivative of the last expectation gives,

\displaystyle\frac{2}{\tau}\text{ }\big(\frac{A_{x}\ket{u}-\ket{u}}{\Delta x}\big)\text{ }\big(\text{ }\big(\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\textbf{1}}{\Delta x}\big)-\big(\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\ket{u}}{\Delta x}\big)\text{ }\big)

\displaystyle\Updownarrow

\displaystyle\frac{2}{\tau}\big(\big(\text{ }\frac{A_{x}\ket{u}-\ket{u}}{\Delta x}\big)\text{ }\big(\text{ }\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\textbf{1}}{\Delta x}\big)-\big(\frac{A_{x}\ket{u}-\ket{u}}{\Delta x}\big)\text{ }\big(\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\ket{u}}{\Delta x}\big)\text{ }\big)

\displaystyle\Updownarrow

\displaystyle\frac{2}{\tau}\text{ }\big(\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-A_{x}\ket{u}-\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-\ket{u}\text{ }\big)\text{ }-\text{ }\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}-\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}+\bra{u}\ket{u}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\big)\text{ }\text{ , }

implying that the desired time evolved variational state is,

\displaystyle\ket{u(t+\tau)}=\tau^{2}\bigg\{\text{ }\big(\text{ }\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\ket{u}-\frac{\alpha}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\text{ }\big(\text{ }A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }-A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }-\cdots

\displaystyle-\big(\text{ }\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\ket{u}\text{ }\big)\text{ }+\text{ }\frac{\beta}{\tau^{2}}\big(\text{ }\big(\text{ }\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)\ket{u(t+2\tau)}\text{ }-\cdots

\displaystyle\big(\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big)\ket{u}\big)\text{ }-\frac{1}{\tau\Delta x}\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u(t+\tau)}\text{ }-2\bra{u}\ket{u(t+\tau)}+\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle 2\bra{u}\ket{u}+\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\big)\text{ }-\frac{2}{\tau(\Delta x)^{2}}\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-A_{x}\ket{u}-\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-\ket{v}-\cdots

\displaystyle\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{v}-2\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}+\bra{u}\ket{v}\text{ }\big)\text{ }+\ket{u}\bigg\}\text{ }

which can be expressed as the cost function whose ground state is parametrized as,

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}^{\mathrm{B-L}}(\lambda_{0},\lambda)=\big|\big|\text{ }\ket{u(t+\tau)}\text{ }-\text{ }\ket{u}\text{ }\big|\big|^{2}=\bigg|\bigg|\text{ }\tau^{2}\bigg\{\text{ }\big(\text{ }\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big)\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{\alpha}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bigg(\text{ }A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }-A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }-\cdots

\displaystyle-\big(\text{ }\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\ket{u}\text{ }\big)\text{ }+\text{ }\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{\beta}{\tau^{2}}\big(\text{ }\big(\text{ }\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)\text{ }\ket{u(t+2\tau)}\text{ }-\text{ }\big(\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\ket{u}\text{ }\big)\text{ }-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\tau\Delta x}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u(t+\tau)}\text{ }-\cdots

\displaystyle 2\bra{u}\ket{u(t+\tau)}+\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle 2\bra{u}\ket{u}+\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\big)\text{ }-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{2}{\tau(\Delta x)^{2}}\bigg(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-A_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-\ket{v}-\cdots

\displaystyle\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{v}-2\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}+\bra{u}\ket{v}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\text{ }\bigg\}\text{ }+\big(\text{ }\tau^{2}-1\text{ }\big)\text{ }\ket{u}\text{ }\text{ }\bigg|\bigg|^{2}\text{ . }

The expansion of the norm abpve is equivalent to,

\displaystyle\bigg|\bigg|\text{ }\tau^{2}\text{ }\big(\text{ }1+\frac{\alpha}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\big(\text{ }\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\ket{u}-\text{ }\tau^{2}\text{ }\bigg\{\text{ }\frac{\alpha}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bigg(\text{ }A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }-A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }\text{ }+\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{\beta}{\tau^{2}}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)\ket{u(t+2\tau)}\text{ }-\text{ }\big(\text{ }\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\ket{u}\text{ }-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\tau\Delta x}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u(t+\tau)}\text{ }-\cdots

\displaystyle 2\bra{u}\ket{u(t+\tau)}+\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle 2\bra{u}\ket{u}+\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\big)\text{ }-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{2}{\tau(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-A_{x}\ket{u}-\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-\ket{v}-\cdots

\displaystyle\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{v}-2\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}+\bra{u}\ket{v}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\bigg\}\text{ }+\big(\text{ }\tau^{2}-1\text{ }\big)\text{ }\ket{u}\text{ }\text{ }\bigg|\bigg|^{2}

To identify expectations in
\mathcal{C}
, we rearrange terms from the following distribution,

\displaystyle\text{ }\bra{u}\big(\text{ }\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big)^{\dagger}\big(\text{ }\tau^{2}\text{ }\big(\text{ }1+\frac{\alpha}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\big)^{2}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\ket{u}\text{ }-\cdots

\displaystyle\tau^{2}\big(\text{ }1+\frac{\alpha}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\big(\text{ }\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\ket{u}\times\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }\big(\tau^{2}\text{ }\text{ }\big\{\text{ }\frac{\alpha}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\text{ }\big(\text{ }A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }-\cdots

\displaystyle A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\text{ }+\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{\beta}{\tau^{2}}\big(\text{ }\text{ }\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)\ket{u(t+2\tau)}\text{ }-\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle\text{ }\big(\text{ }\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big)\ket{u}\text{ }-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\tau\Delta x}\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u(t+\tau)}\text{ }-2\bra{u}\ket{u(t+\tau)}+\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle 2\bra{u}\ket{u}+\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\big)\text{ }-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{2}{\tau(\Delta x)^{2}}\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-A_{x}\ket{u}-\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-\ket{v}-\cdots

\displaystyle A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }-A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\text{ }+\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{\beta}{\tau^{2}}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)\ket{u(t+2\tau)}\text{ }\big\}\text{ }-\text{ }\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle\big(\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big)\ket{u}\bigg)\text{ }-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\tau\Delta x}\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u(t+\tau)}\text{ }-\cdots

\displaystyle 2\bra{u}\ket{u(t+\tau)}+\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle 2\bra{u}\ket{u}+\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\big)\text{ }-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{2}{\tau(\Delta x)^{2}}\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-A_{x}\ket{u}-\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-\ket{v}-\cdots

\displaystyle\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{v}-2\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}+\bra{u}\ket{v}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\bigg\}\text{ }+\big(\text{ }\tau^{2}-1\text{ }\big)\text{ }\ket{u}\text{ }

for the first and second terms of the superposition, and,

\displaystyle\text{ }\bigg\{\text{ }\big(\text{ }\tau^{2}\text{ }\text{ }\bigg\{\text{ }\frac{\alpha}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\big(\text{ }A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }-\cdots

\displaystyle A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\text{ }+\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{\beta}{\tau^{2}}\big(\text{ }\text{ }\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)\text{ }\ket{u(t+2\tau)}\text{ }-\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }\big(\text{ }\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big)\ket{u}\text{ }-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\tau\Delta x}\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u(t+\tau)}\text{ }-2\bra{u}\ket{u(t+\tau)}+\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle 2\bra{u}\ket{u}+\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\big)\text{ }-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{2}{\tau(\Delta x)^{2}}\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-A_{x}\ket{u}-\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }-A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\text{ }+\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{\beta}{\tau^{2}}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)\ket{u(t+2\tau)}\text{ }\bigg\}\text{ }-\text{ }\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle\big(\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big)\ket{u}\big)\text{ }-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\tau\Delta x}\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u(t+\tau)}\text{ }-\cdots

\displaystyle 2\bra{u}\ket{u(t+\tau)}+\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle 2\bra{u}\ket{u}+\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\big)\text{ }-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{2}{\tau(\Delta x)^{2}}\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-A_{x}\ket{u}-\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-\ket{v}-\cdots

\displaystyle\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{v}-2\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}+\bra{u}\ket{v}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\text{ }+\big(\text{ }\tau^{2}-1\text{ }\big)\text{ }\ket{u}\text{ }\bigg\}^{2}\text{ , }

for the third term which is squared. Beginning with one term of the wavefunction, we expand as follows, in which the superposition decomposes as,

\displaystyle\bra{u}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\tau^{2}-1\text{ }\big)^{2}\ket{u}\text{ }\text{ , }\text{ }

corresponding to the last term dependent upon the time step of the evolution
\tau
. Additionally, the remaining terms of the superposition are of the form,

\displaystyle\text{ }\bigg\{\text{ }\big(\text{ }\tau^{2}\text{ }\bigg\{\text{ }\frac{\alpha}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\big(\text{ }A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }-\cdots

\displaystyle A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\text{ }+\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{\beta}{\tau^{2}}\big(\text{ }\text{ }\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)\text{ }\ket{u(t+2\tau)}\text{ }-\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle\text{ }\big(\text{ }\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big)\ket{u}\text{ }-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\tau\Delta x}\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u(t+\tau)}\text{ }-2\bra{u}\ket{u(t+\tau)}+\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle 2\bra{u}\ket{u}+\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\big)\text{ }-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{2}{\tau(\Delta x)^{2}}\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-A_{x}\ket{u}-\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }-A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\text{ }+\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{\beta}{\tau^{2}}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)\text{ }\ket{u(t+2\tau)}\text{ }\bigg\}\text{ }-\cdots

\displaystyle\big(\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\ket{u}\big)\text{ }-\cdots

\displaystyle A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }-A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\text{ }+\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{\beta}{\tau^{2}}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)\ket{u(t+2\tau)}\text{ }\bigg\}\text{ }-\text{ }\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle\big(\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\ket{u}\big)\text{ }-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\tau\Delta x}\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u(t+\tau)}\text{ }-\cdots

\displaystyle 2\bra{u}\ket{u(t+\tau)}+\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle 2\bra{u}\ket{u}+\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\big)\text{ }-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{2}{\tau(\Delta x)^{2}}\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-A_{x}\ket{u}-\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-2\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}+\bra{u}\ket{u}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\bigg\}^{2}\text{ . }\text{ }

The expansion of the squared expectation above takes the form,

\displaystyle\text{ }\tau^{4}\text{ }\bigg\{\text{ }\frac{\alpha}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\text{ }\big(\text{ }A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle 2A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\big)\text{ }+\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle\text{ }\frac{\beta}{\tau^{2}}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\ket{u(t+2\tau)}\text{ }-\big(\text{ }\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\ket{u}\text{ }-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\tau\text{ }\Delta x}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u(t+\tau)}-2\bra{u}\ket{u(t+\tau)}\text{ }+\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }-\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }+\cdots

\displaystyle 2\bra{u}\ket{u}+\text{ }\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\big)\text{ }-\frac{2}{\tau(\Delta x)^{2}\text{ }}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-A_{x}\ket{u}-\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle\ket{u}-A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\big)\text{ }+\frac{\beta}{\tau^{2}}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\ket{u(t+2\tau)}\text{ }\text{ }-\text{ }\big(\text{ }\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\ket{u}\text{ }\bigg\}^{2}\text{ }-\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle\text{ }\bigg\{\text{ }\frac{1}{\tau\Delta x}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u(t+\tau)}-2\bra{u}\ket{u(t+\tau)}+\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle 2\bra{u}\ket{u}+\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\big)-\text{ }\frac{2}{\tau(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-A_{x}\ket{u}-\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle\ket{u}-\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-2\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}+\bra{u}\ket{u}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\bigg\}^{2}\text{ . }

Isolating the first squared quantity gives,

\displaystyle\text{ }\tau^{4}\text{ }\bigg\{\text{ }\frac{\alpha^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{8}}\text{ }\bigg\{\text{ }\big(\text{ }A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }2A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\big)+\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle\text{ }\frac{\beta}{\tau^{2}}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\ket{u(t+2\tau)}\text{ }-\text{ }\big(\text{ }\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\ket{u}\text{ }-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\tau\text{ }\Delta x}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u(t+\tau)}-2\bra{u}\ket{u(t+\tau)}+\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}+2\bra{u}\ket{u}+\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\big)\text{ }-\text{ }\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{2}{\tau\text{ }(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-A_{x}\ket{u}-\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\big)\text{ }+\text{ }\frac{\beta}{\tau^{2}}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\ket{u(t+2\tau)}\text{ }\bigg\}^{2}\text{ , }

while terms of the first square are,

\displaystyle\bra{u}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big)^{\dagger}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\ket{u}\text{ }

\displaystyle\Updownarrow\text{ }

\displaystyle\text{ }\bra{u}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\frac{A_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A^{\dagger}_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\big(\text{ }\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2\ket{u}+A_{x}\ket{u}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big)

\displaystyle\Updownarrow\text{ }

\displaystyle\text{ }\big(\text{ }\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2\ket{u}+A_{x}\ket{u}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\big(\text{ }\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2\ket{u}+A_{x}\ket{u}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big)

\displaystyle\Updownarrow\text{ }

\displaystyle\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}-4\bra{u}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle 2\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle 2\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}+\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }\text{ . }

Finally, remaining expectation values from previous rearrangements are of the form,

\displaystyle-\text{ }\big(\text{ }\frac{\alpha^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{8}}\text{ }\big)^{2}\text{ }\bigg\{\text{ }\big(\text{ }A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }2A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\big)+\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle\text{ }\frac{\beta}{\tau^{2}}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\ket{u(t+2\tau)}\text{ }-\text{ }\text{ }\big(\text{ }\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\ket{u}\text{ }-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\tau\text{ }\Delta x}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u(t+\tau)}-2\bra{u}\ket{u(t+\tau)}+\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}+2\bra{u}\ket{u}+\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\big)\text{ }-\text{ }\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{2}{\tau\text{ }(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-A_{x}\ket{u}-\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\big)\text{ }+\text{ }\frac{\beta}{\tau^{2}}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big)\ket{u(t+2\tau)}\text{ }\bigg\}\text{ }\bigg\{\text{ }\big(\text{ }\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\ket{u}\bigg\}\text{ }\text{ , }

which is rearranged as the following state with distribution, first by distributing the last state given above,

\displaystyle\big(\text{ }\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big)\ket{u}\text{ }\times\text{ }\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle\text{ }\bigg\{\text{ }\big(\text{ }A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }2A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\big)+\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle\text{ }\frac{\beta}{\tau^{2}}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\ket{u(t+2\tau)}\text{ }-\text{ }\text{ }\big(\text{ }\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\ket{u}\text{ }-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\tau\text{ }\Delta x}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u(t+\tau)}-2\bra{u}\ket{u(t+\tau)}+\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}+2\bra{u}\ket{u}+\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\big)\text{ }-\text{ }\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{2}{\tau\text{ }(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-A_{x}\ket{u}-\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\big)\text{ }+\text{ }\frac{\beta}{\tau^{2}}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big)\ket{u(t+2\tau)}\text{ }\bigg\}\text{ }\bigg\{\text{ }\big(\text{ }\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\ket{u}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\Updownarrow

\displaystyle\text{ }\bigg\{\text{ }\big(\text{ }A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }2A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\big)+\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle\text{ }\frac{\beta}{\tau^{2}}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\ket{u(t+2\tau)}\text{ }-\text{ }\text{ }\big(\text{ }\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\ket{u}\text{ }-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\tau\text{ }\Delta x}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u(t+\tau)}-2\bra{u}\ket{u(t+\tau)}+\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}+2\bra{u}\ket{u}+\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\big)\text{ }-\text{ }\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{2}{\tau\text{ }(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-A_{x}\ket{u}-\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-A^{\dagger}_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\big)\text{ }+\text{ }\frac{\beta}{\tau^{2}}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big)\ket{u(t+2\tau)}\text{ }\bigg\}\text{ }\bigg\{\text{ }\big(\text{ }\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\ket{u}\bigg\}\times\text{ }\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }\bra{u}\big(\text{ }\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big)^{\dagger}\text{ . }

The last term given in the superposition above is equivalent to,

\displaystyle\bra{u}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}\text{ }}\big)\equiv\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big(\text{ }A_{x}\ket{u}-2\ket{u}+A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\text{ , }

which is distributed across each expectation in the above superposition. Next, the second squared expectation term leads to the following rearrangements,

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\tau^{2}\text{ }(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u(t+\tau)}-2\bra{u}\ket{u(t+\tau)}+\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}+2\bra{u}\ket{u}+\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\bigg)^{2}\text{ }+\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{4}{\tau^{2}(\Delta x)^{4}}\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-A_{x}\ket{u}-\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle\ket{u}-\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-2\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}+\bra{u}\ket{u}\bigg)^{2}\text{ }-\text{ }\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle\frac{2}{\tau^{2}\text{ }(\Delta x)^{3}}\text{ }\bigg(\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u(t+\tau)}-2\bra{u}\ket{u(t+\tau)}+\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}+2\bra{u}\ket{u}+\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\bigg)\times\text{ }\cdots

\displaystyle\bigg(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-A_{x}\ket{u}-\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle\ket{u}-\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-2\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}+\bra{u}\ket{u}\bigg)-\text{ }\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle\frac{2}{\tau^{2}(\Delta x)^{3}\text{ }}\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-A_{x}\ket{u}-\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle\ket{u}-\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-2\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}+\bra{u}\ket{u}\bigg)\times\text{ }\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle\bigg(\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u(t+\tau)}-2\bra{u}\ket{u(t+\tau)}+\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}+2\bra{u}\ket{u}+\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }\text{ . }

Directly from the superposition above, term by term expanding the first squared expectation quantity yields,

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\tau^{2}\text{ }(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u(t+\tau)}-2\bra{u}\ket{u(t+\tau)}+\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}+2\bra{u}\ket{u}+\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\bigg)^{2}

\displaystyle\Updownarrow

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\tau^{2}\text{ }(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u(t+\tau)}\text{ }\big)^{\dagger}\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u(t+\tau)}-\text{ }\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }2\bra{u}\ket{u(t+\tau)}-\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle 2\bra{u}\ket{u}-\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\bigg)^{2}-\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle\text{ }\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u(t+\tau)}\text{ }\text{ }\big)\text{ }\big(\text{ }-2\bra{u}\ket{u(t+\tau)}\text{ }+\text{ }\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}+2\bra{u}\ket{u}+\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\bigg)-\text{ }\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }\bigg(\text{ }-2\bra{u}\ket{u(t+\tau)}\text{ }+\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle 2\bra{u}\ket{u}\text{ }+\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }\times\cdots

\displaystyle\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u(t+\tau)}\text{ }\big)\text{ }

\displaystyle\frac{2}{\tau^{2}(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\big(\text{ }A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\big)\text{ }+\text{ }\frac{2}{\tau^{2}(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\big(\text{ }\ket{u}\text{ }\big)\text{ }+\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }\frac{2}{\tau^{2}(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\big)\text{ }+\text{ }\frac{4}{\tau^{2}(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\big)\text{ }-\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }\frac{2}{\tau^{2}(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\bra{u}\ket{u}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\big(\text{ }A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\big)\text{ }-\text{ }\frac{4}{\tau^{2}(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\bra{u}\ket{u}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\big)-\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }\text{ }\frac{2}{\tau^{2}(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\big(\text{ }A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\big)\text{ }+\frac{2}{\tau^{2}(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\big)-\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle\text{ }\frac{4}{\tau^{2}(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\big)\text{ }+\frac{2}{\tau^{2}(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\big(\text{ }\bra{u}\ket{u}\text{ }\big)\text{ }+\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }\frac{2}{\tau^{2}(\Delta x)^{3}}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{\widetilde{u}}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\big)-\text{ }\frac{2}{\tau^{2}(\Delta x)^{3}}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{\widetilde{u}}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\big(\text{ }A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\big)-\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }\text{ }\frac{2}{\tau^{2}(\Delta x)^{3}}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{\widetilde{u}}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\big)\text{ }+\frac{2}{\tau^{2}(\Delta x)^{3}}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{\widetilde{u}}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\big(\text{ }\ket{u}\text{ }\big)\text{ }+\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }\frac{2}{\tau^{2}(\Delta x)^{3}}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{\widetilde{u}}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\bigg)\text{ }+\text{ }\frac{4}{\tau^{2}(\Delta x)^{3}}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{\widetilde{u}}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\big)\text{ }-\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }\text{ }\frac{2}{\tau^{2}(\Delta x)^{3}}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{\widetilde{u}}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\big(\text{ }\bra{u}\ket{u}\text{ }\big)\text{ }-\text{ }\frac{4}{\tau^{2}(\Delta x)^{3}}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\bra{u}\ket{\widetilde{u}}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\big)-\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }\frac{2}{\tau^{2}(\Delta x)^{3}}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\big)\text{ }+\frac{2}{\tau^{2}(\Delta x)^{3}}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\big(\text{ }A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\big)\text{ }+\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }\frac{2}{\tau^{2}(\Delta x)^{3}}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\big)\text{ }+\frac{4}{\tau^{2}(\Delta x)^{3}}\text{ }\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\big)\text{ }\big(\text{ }\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\big)-\cdots

following the expansion of all previously mentioned terms.

4.18
Airy equation

Cost function derivation

The equation reads,

\displaystyle u_{t}=-u_{xxx}\text{ }\text{ , }

which, from previous steps can yield a corresponding cost function, from the fact that,

\displaystyle\ket{\widetilde{u}}=\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }A_{x}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\ket{u}

\displaystyle\equiv\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big(A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2A_{x}\ket{u}+A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}\big)\text{ }\text{ , }

implying,

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}^{\mathrm{A}}(\lambda_{0},\lambda)=\big|\big|\ket{\widetilde{u}}-\ket{u}\big|^{2}\big|

\displaystyle=\bigg|\bigg|\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big(A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2A_{x}\ket{u}+A_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}\big)-\ket{u}\bigg|\bigg|^{2}

\displaystyle\approx\lambda_{0}^{2}+\big(\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\big(A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2A_{x}\ket{u}+\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}\big)^{\dagger}\text{ }\big(A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2A_{x}\ket{u}+\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}\big)+\bra{u}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\bra{u}\big(A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2A_{x}\ket{u}+\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}\big)\text{ }

\displaystyle\approx\lambda_{0}^{2}+\big(\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-2A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}\big)\text{ }\big(A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2A_{x}\ket{u}+\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}\big)+\bra{u}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big(\bra{u}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2A_{x}\ket{u}+\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}\big)\text{ }

\displaystyle\approx\lambda_{0}^{2}+\big(\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\big(\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}-2\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}A_{x}\ket{u}+\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{3}A_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle 2\bra{u}A_{x}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}+4\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}-2\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{3}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle\bra{u}A_{x}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{3}\ket{u}-2\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{3}\ket{u}+\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{4}\ket{u}\big)+\bra{u}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\text{ }\big(\bra{u}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2A_{x}\ket{u}+\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}\big)\text{ }\text{ . }

As a result,

\displaystyle\boxed{\mathcal{C}^{\mathrm{A}}(\lambda_{0},\lambda)}\approx\lambda_{0}^{2}+\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}-\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}A_{x}\ket{u}+\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{3}A_{x}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{4}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}-\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{3}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A_{x}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{3}\ket{u}+\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{4}\ket{u}+\bra{u}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\frac{4}{(\Delta x)^{4}}A_{x}\ket{u}+\frac{2}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}\text{ }\text{ . }

Measurement extraction from the Airy cost function

4.19
Generalized Camassa-Holm equation with a quadratic order nonlinearity

Cost function derivation

The generalized equation reads, for quadratic order,

\displaystyle u_{t}=\epsilon u_{xxt}+3uu_{x}-2\epsilon^{2}u_{x}u_{xx}-\epsilon^{2}uu_{xxx}\text{ }\text{ , }

which has the following quantum representation,

\displaystyle\ket{\widetilde{u}}=\frac{\epsilon}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big(\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\ket{u}\big)_{t}+3\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}-2\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\big(A_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)A_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\big(A_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)\ket{u}

\displaystyle\Updownarrow

\displaystyle\big(1+\frac{\epsilon}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)\ket{\widetilde{u}}=\frac{\epsilon}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big(\tilde{A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}}+\tilde{A_{x}\ket{u}}\big)+3\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}-2\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}+4\times\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\ket{u}-2\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-2\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}\text{ }\text{ , }

implying that the cost function would then take the form,

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}^{\mathrm{quadratic}}(\lambda_{0},\lambda)=\big|\big|\ket{\widetilde{u}}-\ket{u}\big|\big|^{2}

\displaystyle=\bigg|\bigg|\frac{\epsilon}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big(\tilde{A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}}+\tilde{A_{x}\ket{u}}\big)+3\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}-2\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}+4\times\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\ket{u}-2\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-2\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}\text{ }-\ket{u}\bigg|\bigg|^{2}

\displaystyle\approx\bigg|\bigg|\frac{\epsilon}{(\Delta x)^{2}}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{\widetilde{u}}-\frac{\epsilon}{(\Delta x)^{2}}A_{x}\ket{\widetilde{u}}+3\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle 2\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}+4\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\ket{u}-3\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle 2\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}-\ket{u}\bigg|\bigg|

\displaystyle\approx\lambda^{2}_{0}+\big(\frac{\epsilon}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\bra{\widetilde{u}}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\underset{(*)}{\underbrace{\big(\frac{\epsilon}{(\Delta x)^{2}}A_{x}\ket{\widetilde{u}}+\cdots}}

\displaystyle\underset{(*)}{\underbrace{3\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}-2\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}+4\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\ket{u}-\cdots}}

\displaystyle\underset{(*)}{\underbrace{3\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-2\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\cdots}}

\displaystyle\underset{(*)}{\underbrace{\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}-\ket{u}\big)^{2}}}+\underset{(**)}{\underbrace{2\big(\frac{\epsilon}{(\Delta x)^{2}}A_{x}\ket{\widetilde{u}}+\cdots}}

\displaystyle\underset{(**)}{\underbrace{3\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}-2\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}+4\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\ket{u}-3\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots}}

\displaystyle\underset{(**)}{\underbrace{2\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}-\ket{u}\big)\text{ }\big(\frac{\epsilon}{(\Delta x)^{2}}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{\widetilde{u}}\big)}}\text{ }\text{ . }

Proceeding, for
(*)
,

\displaystyle(*)\approx\big(\frac{\epsilon}{(\Delta x)^{2}}A_{x}\ket{\widetilde{u}}+3\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}-2\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}+4\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\ket{u}\big)^{2}+2\big(-3\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots\text{ }

\displaystyle 2\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}-\ket{u}\big)\big(\frac{\epsilon}{(\Delta x)^{2}}A_{x}\ket{\widetilde{u}}+3\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}-2\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle 4\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\ket{u}\big)+\big(-3\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-2\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}-\ket{u}\big)^{2}

\displaystyle\approx\big(\frac{\epsilon}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\bra{\widetilde{u}}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{\widetilde{u}}+\big(3\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}-2\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}+4\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\ket{u}\big)^{2}+\cdots

\displaystyle 2\big(-2\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}+4\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\ket{u}\big)\times\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }\big(\frac{\epsilon}{(\Delta x)^{2}}A_{x}\ket{\widetilde{u}}+3\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}\big)+\cdots

\displaystyle 2\big(-3\frac{\epsilon^{3}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{\widetilde{u}}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}A_{x}\ket{u}-9\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle 6\frac{\epsilon^{4}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}

\displaystyle\approx\big(\frac{\epsilon}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)\bra{\widetilde{u}}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{\widetilde{u}}+9\bra{u}\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}+\underset{(***)}{\underbrace{\big(-2\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}|\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}+\cdots}}

\displaystyle\underset{(***)}{\underbrace{4\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\ket{u}\big)^{2}}}+2\big(3\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}\big)\text{ }\big(-2\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}|\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}+4\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\ket{u}\big)+\cdots

\displaystyle 2\big(-2\frac{\epsilon^{3}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}\big(A_{x}\big)^{3}\ket{\widetilde{u}}-6\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\big(A_{x}\big)^{3}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle 4\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{\widetilde{u}}+12\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}\big)+\cdots

\displaystyle 2\big(-3\frac{\epsilon^{3}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{\widetilde{u}}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}A_{x}\ket{u}-9\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle 6\frac{\epsilon^{4}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\big)\text{ }\text{ , }

where expanding the remaining terms above implies,

\displaystyle(***)\approx 4\big(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\bra{u}\big(A_{x}\big)^{4}\ket{u}+16\big(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\bra{u}\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}+\big(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\bra{u}\ket{u}\text{ }\text{ , }

while, also by direct computation, a previous term in the expansion satisfies,

\displaystyle(**)\approx 2\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{\widetilde{u}}\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{\widetilde{u}}+3\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{\widetilde{u}}-2\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\big(A_{x}\big)^{3}\ket{\widetilde{u}}+4\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{\widetilde{u}}A_{x}\ket{u}-3\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\times\cdots

\displaystyle\bra{u}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-2\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{\widetilde{u}}+\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A_{x}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{\widetilde{u}}-\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{\widetilde{u}}A_{x}\ket{u}\big)

\displaystyle\approx 2\big(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\bra{\widetilde{u}}\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{\widetilde{u}}+6\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{\widetilde{u}}-4\big(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\bra{u}\big(A_{x}\big)^{3}\ket{\widetilde{u}}+\cdots

\displaystyle 8\big(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\bra{\widetilde{u}}A_{x}\ket{u}-6\big(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\bra{u}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-4\big(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\bra{u}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{\widetilde{u}}+\cdots

\displaystyle 2\big(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\bra{u}A_{x}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{\widetilde{u}}-2\big(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\bra{\widetilde{u}}A_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\text{ , }

yielding,

\displaystyle\boxed{\mathcal{C}^{\mathrm{quadratic}}(\lambda_{0},\lambda)}\approx\lambda^{2}_{0}+\mathrm{Re}\big\{\big(\frac{\epsilon}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\bra{\widetilde{u}}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\big(\frac{\epsilon}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)\bra{\widetilde{u}}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{\widetilde{u}}-9\bra{u}\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle 12\big(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\bra{u}\big(A_{x}\big)^{4}\ket{u}+24\big(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\bra{u}\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}+8\big(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\bra{u}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle 4\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\big(A_{x}\big)^{3}\ket{u}+12\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}+8\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{\widetilde{u}}+24\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle 6\frac{\epsilon^{3}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{\widetilde{u}}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}A_{x}\ket{u}-18\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}+12\frac{\epsilon^{4}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}+\cdots

\displaystyle 4\big(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\bra{u}\big(A_{x}\big)^{4}\ket{u}+16\big(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\bra{u}\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}+\big(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\bra{u}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle 2\big(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\bra{\widetilde{u}}\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{\widetilde{u}}+6\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{\widetilde{u}}-4\big(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\bra{u}\big(A_{x}\big)^{3}\ket{\widetilde{u}}+\cdots

\displaystyle 8\big(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\bra{\widetilde{u}}A_{x}\ket{u}-6\big(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\bra{u}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}A_{x}\ket{u}-4\big(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\bra{u}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{\widetilde{u}}+\cdots

\displaystyle 2\big(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\bra{u}A_{x}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{\widetilde{u}}-2\big(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\bra{\widetilde{u}}A_{x}\ket{u}\big\}\text{ }\text{ . }

4.20
Generalized Camassa-Holm equation with a cubic order nonlinearity

Cost function derivation

The generalized equation reads, given
\epsilon\in\textbf{C}
, for cubic order,

\displaystyle-\epsilon^{2}u_{xxt}+\bigg(\epsilon^{2}u^{2}u_{xxx}+3\epsilon^{2}uu_{x}u_{xx}-4u^{2}u_{x}\bigg)=u_{t}\text{ }\text{ , }

which, upon passing to a quantum representative of the solution state that is time-evolved, approximately satisfies,

\displaystyle-\epsilon^{2}\big(\big(\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)\ket{u}\big)_{t}+\bigg(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}-2\textbf{1}+A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)\ket{u^{2}}+3\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\ket{u}A_{x}\ket{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{4}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\ket{u^{2}}\bigg)=\big(1-\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)\ket{\widetilde{u}}\text{ }\text{ , }

implying,

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}^{\mathrm{cubic}}(\lambda_{0},\lambda)=\big|\big|\ket{\widetilde{u}}-\ket{u}\big|\big|^{2}

\displaystyle=\bigg|\bigg|\text{ }\big(1-\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{-1}\text{ }\bigg(-\epsilon^{2}\big(\big(\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)\ket{u}\big)_{t}+\bigg(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}-2\textbf{1}+A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)\ket{u^{2}}+3\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\ket{u}A_{x}\ket{u}\times\cdots

\displaystyle\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\ket{u}-\frac{4}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\ket{u^{2}}\bigg)\bigg)-\ket{u}\bigg|\bigg|

\displaystyle\approx\bigg|\bigg|\big(1-\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{-1}\text{ }\bigg(-\epsilon^{2}\underset{(*)}{\underbrace{\big(\big(\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)\ket{u}\big)_{t}}}+\bigg(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u^{2}}-2\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\ket{u^{2}}+\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+3\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}\big)\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{4}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u^{2}}-\frac{8}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\ket{u}+\frac{4}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u^{2}}\bigg)\bigg)-\ket{u}\bigg|\bigg|

where, for the quantum state associated with
(*)
above, we denote the time evolved variational state for the second derivative of solutions
u
by making use of the fact that,

\displaystyle\big(\big(\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)\ket{u}\big)_{t}\equiv\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-2\ket{u}+A_{x}\ket{u}\big)_{t}\equiv\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big(\tilde{A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}}-\ket{\widetilde{u}}+\tilde{A_{x}\ket{u}}\big)\text{ }\text{ , }

implying,

\displaystyle\big(\big(\frac{A^{\dagger}_{x}-2\textbf{1}+A_{x}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)\ket{u}\big)_{t}\equiv\frac{1}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big(\tilde{A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}}+\tilde{A_{x}\ket{u}}\big)\text{ }\text{ , }

for unitary matrices
U
satisfying,

\displaystyle\tilde{A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}}=UA^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\text{ , }

\displaystyle\tilde{A_{x}\ket{u}}=UA_{x}\ket{u}\text{ }\text{ , }

hence yielding, approximately,

\displaystyle\big(1-\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{-2}\text{ }\bigg|\bigg|\frac{-\epsilon}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\tilde{A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}}-\frac{\epsilon}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\tilde{A_{x}\ket{u}}+\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u^{2}}-2\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\ket{u^{2}}+\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+3\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}-6\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+3\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{4}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u^{2}}-\frac{8}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\ket{u}+\frac{4}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u^{2}}-\ket{u}\bigg|\bigg|

\displaystyle\approx\lambda^{2}_{0}\text{ }+\text{ }\big(1-\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{-2}\text{ }\bigg(\big(\frac{-\epsilon}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\tilde{A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}}-\frac{\epsilon}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\tilde{A_{x}\ket{u}}+\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u^{2}}-2\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\ket{u^{2}}+\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+3\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}-6\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+3\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{4}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u^{2}}\big)^{2}+\big(\frac{8}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\ket{u}-\frac{4}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u^{2}}\big)^{2}+\cdots

\displaystyle 2\big(\frac{-\epsilon}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\tilde{A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}}-\frac{\epsilon}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\tilde{A_{x}\ket{u}}+\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u^{2}}-2\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\ket{u^{2}}+\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+3\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}-6\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+3\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{4}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u^{2}}\big)^{2}+\big(\frac{8}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\ket{u}-\frac{4}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u^{2}}\big)\times\cdots

\displaystyle\big(\frac{8}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\ket{u}-\frac{4}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u^{2}}\big)\bigg)

As a result,

\displaystyle\boxed{\mathcal{C}^{\mathrm{cubic}}(\lambda_{0},\lambda)}\approx\lambda^{2}_{0}\text{ }+\text{ }\mathrm{Re}\big\{\big(1-\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{-2}\text{ }\big(2\big(\frac{8}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\bra{u}\ket{u}-2\big(\frac{32}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u^{2}}\big)+\frac{16}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u^{2}}+\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{-\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{\widetilde{u}}A_{x}\ket{\widetilde{u}}-2\frac{\epsilon}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{\widetilde{u}}\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{\widetilde{u}}+\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{\widetilde{u}}\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{\widetilde{u}}+\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{\epsilon^{4}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u^{2}}+2\frac{\epsilon^{4}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u^{2}}+4\frac{\epsilon^{4}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}\ket{u^{2}}+\cdots

\displaystyle 2\big(\frac{-\epsilon^{3}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{\widetilde{u}}+2\frac{\epsilon^{3}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u^{2}}-\frac{\epsilon^{3}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u^{2}}+\cdots

\displaystyle 2\frac{\epsilon^{3}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u^{2}}\big)+\frac{\epsilon^{4}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}+6\frac{\epsilon^{3}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{3}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle 9\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{4}\ket{u}+2\big(-6\frac{\epsilon^{4}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}-18\frac{\epsilon^{3}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{3}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle 36\frac{\epsilon^{6}}{(\Delta x)^{6}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{3}\ket{u}+108\frac{\epsilon^{5}}{(\Delta x)^{6}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{4}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle 2\big(\frac{-\epsilon^{3}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{\widetilde{u}}-3\frac{\epsilon^{3}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A_{x}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{\widetilde{u}}+6\frac{\epsilon^{3}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{\widetilde{u}}-\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{\epsilon^{3}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{\widetilde{u}}-3\frac{\epsilon^{3}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A_{x}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{\widetilde{u}}+6\frac{\epsilon^{3}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{\widetilde{u}}+\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{\epsilon^{4}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u^{2}}+3\frac{\epsilon^{4}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u^{2}}-6\frac{\epsilon^{4}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u^{2}}-\cdots

\displaystyle 2\frac{\epsilon^{4}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+6\frac{\epsilon^{4}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}-12\frac{\epsilon^{4}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\big)+\cdots

\displaystyle\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{\widetilde{u}}A_{x}\ket{\widetilde{u}}+\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u^{2}}+\cdots

\displaystyle\ket{u}\big)+\big(2\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\big)^{2}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u^{2}}-2\big(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\big)^{2}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u^{2}}-\cdots

\displaystyle 3\big(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u^{2}}+\cdots

\displaystyle 2\big(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\bra{u}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u^{2}}-4\big(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\bra{\widetilde{u}}A_{x}\ket{u^{2}}-2\big(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\bra{\widetilde{u}}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle 6\big(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\bra{\widetilde{u}}A_{x}\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}+4\big(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\bra{u}\ket{u^{2}}-\big(\big(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle 2\big(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{3}\ket{u}+9\big(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{4}\ket{u}\big)+\cdots

\displaystyle 4\big(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u^{2}}+\cdots

\displaystyle 12\big(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}\big)\big)\big\}\text{ }\text{ , }

because the second squared term appearing in the superposition above satisfies,

\displaystyle\big(\frac{8}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\ket{u}-\frac{4}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u^{2}}\big)^{2}\approx\big(\frac{8}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\bra{u}\ket{u}-2\big(\big(\frac{8}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\ket{u}\big)\text{ }\big(-\frac{4}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u^{2}}\big)\big)+\cdots

\displaystyle\big(-\frac{4}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u^{2}}\big)^{2}

\displaystyle\approx\big(\frac{8}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\bra{u}\ket{u}-2\big(\frac{32}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u^{2}}\big)+\frac{16}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u^{2}}\text{ }\text{ , }

while, also from the expansion above, the first squared term satisfies,

\displaystyle\big(\frac{-\epsilon}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\tilde{A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}}-\frac{\epsilon}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\tilde{A_{x}\ket{u}}+\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u^{2}}-2\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\ket{u^{2}}+\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}

\displaystyle+3\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}

\displaystyle-6\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+3\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\frac{4}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u^{2}}\big)^{2}

\displaystyle\approx\big(\frac{-\epsilon}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\tilde{A_{x}\ket{u}}-\frac{\epsilon}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\tilde{A_{x}\ket{u}}+\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u^{2}}-2\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\ket{u^{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+3\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}-6\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\big)^{2}+\cdots

\displaystyle\big(3\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\frac{4}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\big)^{2}

\displaystyle+2\big(\frac{-\epsilon}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\tilde{A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}}-\frac{\epsilon}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\tilde{A_{x}\ket{u}}+\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u^{2}}-2\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\ket{u^{2}}+\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}

\displaystyle+3\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}-6\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\big)\text{ }\big(3\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\frac{4}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u^{2}}\big)\text{ }\text{ , }

which is equivalent to, after further simplification,

\displaystyle\big(\frac{-\epsilon}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\tilde{A_{x}\ket{u}}-\frac{\epsilon}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\tilde{A_{x}\ket{u}}+\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u^{2}}-2\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\ket{u^{2}}\big)^{2}+

\displaystyle\big(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+3\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}-6\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\big)^{2}

\displaystyle+2\big(\frac{-\epsilon}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\tilde{A_{x}\ket{u}}-\frac{\epsilon}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\tilde{A_{x}\ket{u}}+\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u^{2}}

\displaystyle-2\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\ket{u^{2}}\big)\text{ }\big(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+3\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}-6\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\big)

\displaystyle\approx\big(\frac{-\epsilon}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\tilde{A_{x}\ket{u}}-\frac{\epsilon}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\tilde{A_{x}\ket{u}}\big)^{2}+\big(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u^{2}}-2\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\ket{u^{2}}\big)^{2}+2\big(\frac{-\epsilon}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\tilde{A_{x}\ket{u}}-\frac{\epsilon}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\tilde{A_{x}\ket{u}}\big)

\displaystyle\times\text{ }\big(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u^{2}}-2\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\ket{u^{2}}\big)

\displaystyle+\big(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+3\frac{\epsilon}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}\big)^{2}+2\big(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+3\frac{\epsilon}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}\big)\text{ }

\displaystyle\times\big(-6\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\big)\text{ }+\text{ }36\frac{\epsilon^{4}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}\big)

\displaystyle+2\big(\frac{-\epsilon^{3}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{\widetilde{u}}-3\frac{\epsilon^{3}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A_{x}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{\widetilde{u}}+6\frac{\epsilon^{3}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{\widetilde{u}}

\displaystyle-\frac{\epsilon^{3}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{\widetilde{u}}-3\frac{\epsilon^{3}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A_{x}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{\widetilde{u}}+6\frac{\epsilon^{3}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{\widetilde{u}}

\displaystyle+\frac{\epsilon^{4}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u^{2}}+3\frac{\epsilon^{4}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u^{2}}-6\frac{\epsilon^{4}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u^{2}}

\displaystyle-2\frac{\epsilon^{4}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+6\frac{\epsilon^{4}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}-12\frac{\epsilon^{4}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\big)\text{ }\text{ . }

Proceeding,

\displaystyle\approx\frac{-\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{\widetilde{u}}A_{x}\ket{\widetilde{u}}-2\frac{\epsilon}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{\widetilde{u}}\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{\widetilde{u}}+\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{\widetilde{u}}\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{\widetilde{u}}

\displaystyle+\frac{\epsilon^{4}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u^{2}}+2\frac{\epsilon^{4}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u^{2}}+4\frac{\epsilon^{4}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}\ket{u^{2}}

\displaystyle+2\big(\frac{-\epsilon^{3}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{\widetilde{u}}+2\frac{\epsilon^{3}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u^{2}}-\frac{\epsilon^{3}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u^{2}}

\displaystyle+2\frac{\epsilon^{3}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A_{x}\ket{u^{2}}\big)+\frac{\epsilon^{4}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}+6\frac{\epsilon^{3}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{3}\ket{u}

\displaystyle+9\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{4}\ket{u}+2\big(-6\frac{\epsilon^{4}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}-18\frac{\epsilon^{3}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{3}\ket{u}

\displaystyle+36\frac{\epsilon^{6}}{(\Delta x)^{6}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{3}\ket{u}+108\frac{\epsilon^{5}}{(\Delta x)^{6}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{4}\ket{u}

\displaystyle+2\big(\frac{-\epsilon^{3}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{\widetilde{u}}-3\frac{\epsilon^{3}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A_{x}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{\widetilde{u}}+6\frac{\epsilon^{3}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{\widetilde{u}}

\displaystyle-\frac{\epsilon^{3}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{\widetilde{u}}-3\frac{\epsilon^{3}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A_{x}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{\widetilde{u}}+6\frac{\epsilon^{3}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{\widetilde{u}}

\displaystyle+\frac{\epsilon^{4}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u^{2}}+3\frac{\epsilon^{4}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u^{2}}-6\frac{\epsilon^{4}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u^{2}}

\displaystyle-2\frac{\epsilon^{4}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+6\frac{\epsilon^{4}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}-12\frac{\epsilon^{4}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}\big)\text{ }\text{ , }

corresponds to the superposition of the expansion of the cross term. On the other hand, the second squared term satisfies,

\displaystyle 2\big(\frac{-\epsilon}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{\widetilde{u}}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{\widetilde{u}}+\big(\underset{(*)}{\underbrace{\frac{\epsilon}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\tilde{A_{x}\ket{u}}-\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u^{2}}+2\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\ket{u^{2}}-\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots}}

\displaystyle\underset{(*)}{\underbrace{3\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}+6\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-3\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\frac{4}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u^{2}}\big)^{2}}}+\cdots

\displaystyle 2\big(\frac{-\epsilon}{(\Delta x)^{2}}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{\widetilde{u}}\big)\text{ }\big(\frac{\epsilon}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\tilde{A_{x}\ket{u}}-\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u^{2}}+2\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\ket{u^{2}}-\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle 3\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}+6\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-3\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\frac{4}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u^{2}}\big)\big)\text{ }\text{ , }

where the expectation values in
(*)
above satisfy,

\displaystyle\big(\frac{\epsilon}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\tilde{A_{x}\ket{u}}-\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u^{2}}+2\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\ket{u^{2}}-\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-3\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}\big)^{2}+\cdots

\displaystyle\big(6\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-3\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\frac{4}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u^{2}}\big)^{2}+2\times\cdots

\displaystyle\big(\frac{\epsilon}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\tilde{A_{x}\ket{u}}-\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u^{2}}+2\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\ket{u^{2}}-\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-3\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}\big)\times\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }\big(6\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-3\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\frac{4}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u^{2}}\big)

\displaystyle\approx\text{ }\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{\widetilde{u}}A_{x}\ket{\widetilde{u}}+\big(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u^{2}}-2\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\ket{u^{2}}+\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle 3\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}\big)^{2}+\cdots

\displaystyle 2\big(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{\widetilde{u}}A_{x}\ket{\widetilde{u}}\big)\text{ }\big(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u^{2}}-\cdots

\displaystyle 2\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\ket{u^{2}}+\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+3\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}\big)

\displaystyle\approx\text{ }\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{\widetilde{u}}A_{x}\ket{\widetilde{u}}+\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u^{2}}-\underset{(**)}{\underbrace{\big(2\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\ket{u^{2}}-\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots}}

\displaystyle\underset{(**)}{\underbrace{3\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}\big)^{2}}}+2\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{4}}\big(\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u^{2}}\big)\text{ }\big(2\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\ket{u^{2}}-\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}-\cdots

\displaystyle 3\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}\big)+\cdots

\displaystyle 2\big(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\bra{u}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u^{2}}-4\big(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\bra{\widetilde{u}}A_{x}\ket{u^{2}}-2\big(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\bra{\widetilde{u}}A_{x}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle 6\big(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\bra{\widetilde{u}}A_{x}\big(A_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}\text{ }\text{ , }

where,
(**)
satisfies,

\displaystyle(**)\approx 4\big(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\bra{u}\ket{u^{2}}-\big(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+3\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}\big)^{2}+4\big(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\ket{u^{2}}\big)\times\cdots

\displaystyle\text{ }\big(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u}+3\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}\big)

\displaystyle\approx 4\big(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\bra{u}\ket{u^{2}}-\big(\big(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}+2\big(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{3}\ket{u}+\cdots

\displaystyle 9\big(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{4}\ket{u}\big)+4\big(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\bra{u}A^{\dagger}_{x}\ket{u^{2}}+\cdots

\displaystyle 12\big(\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{(\Delta x)^{2}}\big)^{2}\bra{u}\big(A^{\dagger}_{x}\big)^{2}\ket{u}\text{ }\text{ , }

hence yielding the desired cost function of cubic order.
